### [66] [Trend strength predicts when generative foundation models win: a power-controlled benchmark, a mechanism, and an actionable selection rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19383)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 零样本Chronos基础模型在时间序列预测中优于经典方法，但其优势源于趋势收缩效应而非更好的趋势外推，且趋势强度可作为是否使用该模型的先验选择指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练生成式基础模型在零样本时间序列预测中取得成功的机制原因，并将其转化为可操作的理解，以指导实际部署决策。

Method: 通过三项分析建立机制性理解：(1)对1728次滚动原点预测进行功率控制基准测试，比较Chronos与四种经典基线；(2)设计受控合成实验，使用已知趋势生成过程量化Chronos的趋势外推行为；(3)基于趋势强度提出可操作的选择规则。

Result: (1) Chronos显著优于经典基线，最佳均值MASE 1.187（Theta 1.337，ETS 1.656；Friedman检验p=8.75e-09）；(2) Chronos表现为趋势收缩估计器，系统性欠外推（线性/阻尼/指数趋势下斜率跟踪比分别为0.80/0.49/0.36），而ETS准确跟踪趋势；(3)低趋势系列中Chronos获胜率78%，高趋势系列仅44%；(4)校准不足，80%区间实际覆盖率仅0.77。

Conclusion: 基础模型的预测优势来自趋势收缩效应而非更好的趋势建模，趋势强度是可从训练上下文计算的实用先验指标，但模型存在校准不足问题。

Abstract: Pretrained generative foundation models cast forecasting as conditional generation from a learned predictive distribution and forecast unseen series zero-shot. We establish three results that turn their reported success into an actionable, mechanistic understanding. First (a positive benchmark result): on a power-controlled study of 1728 rolling-origin forecasts over 36 series from 19 datasets spanning the full range of STL trend strength (F_T in [0.17, 1.00]), a zero-shot Chronos model significantly outperforms four strong classical baselines -- drift, seasonal-naive, Theta, and additive Holt-Winters/ETS -- with the best mean MASE (1.187 vs. Theta 1.337, ETS 1.656; Friedman chi^2 = 46.08, p = 8.75e-09; Holm-corrected Wilcoxon p <= 0.015 against every baseline; a Nemenyi critical difference separating it from the classical pack). Second (a novel, quantified mechanism): a controlled synthetic experiment with a known trend-generating process shows why -- and reveals that the win does not come from better trend extrapolation. When the true trend is linear, damped, or exponential, additive ETS tracks the slope (slope-tracking ratio 1.02, 1.34, 0.98) whereas Chronos systematically under-extrapolates, behaving as a trend-shrinkage estimator (ratio 0.80, 0.49, 0.36). Third (an actionable selection rule): because the advantage is a shrinkage effect, it is predictable from trend strength alone -- the generative model wins 78% of low-trend series but only 44% of high-trend ones, and its edge over ETS is significant on the low-trend stratum (0.982 vs. 1.671, p = 0.002) yet a tie on the high-trend stratum (p = 0.18). Trend strength, computable before forecasting from the training context alone, is therefore a practical a-priori indicator of when to deploy a foundation model. We additionally document a calibration shortfall (80% intervals cover 0.77).

</details>


### [67] [Interrupted Time Series Analysis Of Count Data With Nuisance Interruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19493)
*Benjamin Stockton,Linda G. Kahn,Shilpi S. Mehta-Lee,Erinn M. Hade*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出使用贝叶斯堆叠方法，通过在干扰事件（如COVID-19）上考虑多种函数形式，来改进中断时间序列分析中反事实预测的稳健性，并以2021年得州六周堕胎禁令对怀孕率的影响为例进行了实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的中断时间序列分析仅依赖单一研究单位，难以调整干预期间其他同时发生的干扰事件（如COVID-19疫情），导致反事实预测存在偏差。

Method: 采用贝叶斯堆叠（Bayesian stacking）方法，对干扰事件（COVID-19）的影响考虑多种函数形式，从而对干预期进行更稳健的反事实预测。

Result: 成功应用该方法估计了2021年得州六周堕胎禁令对得州女性记录在案的怀孕率的影响，同时调整了COVID-19疫情的干扰。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯堆叠能够有效处理中断时间序列分析中由干扰事件带来的混杂问题，提高政策效果评估的准确性。

Abstract: Interrupted time series analysis has been used to model the effect of policy and other interventions on public health by forecasting a counterfactual time series during the intervention period using data from prior to the intervention. However, due to typically relying on a single study unit, this approach risks not adjusting for other interruptions that precede and co-occur during the intervention period. The COVID-19 pandemic is a prominent example of this phenomenon of nuisance interruptions in contemporary public health research. To address this complication, we propose using Bayesian stacking over a range of functional forms for the impact of the nuisance interruption in order to make counterfactual forecasts for the intervention period. We used our proposed methods to estimate the impact of the 2021 Texas six-week abortion ban on documented pregnancies among women in Texas while adjusting for the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Publication-Bias Detection: Estimating Bias under Uncertainty in Heterogeneous Literatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19626)
*Ulrich Schimmack*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模模拟研究证明，发表偏倚检验在效果量异质且数据不符合模型假设时统计功效很低，即使有1000项研究也难以检测出偏倚。作者主张从检验偏倚转向用置信区间估计偏倚，发现阶梯函数选择模型的置信区间过窄，而z-curve通过将预期发现率转换为选择权重参数得到的置信区间具有名义覆盖率，是检验发表偏倚的有用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统发表偏倚检验在异质性高时功效极低（即使有1000项研究），不显著的结果无法区分是真无偏倚还是II类错误（假阴性）。

Method: 大规模析因模拟研究，比较阶梯函数选择模型与z-curve的置信区间覆盖率；并用社会启动效应和应用心理学元分析进行实证说明。

Result: 1) 发表偏倚检验在异质性高时功效很低；2) 阶梯函数选择模型的置信区间过窄，真实偏倚量仅约50%时间被覆盖；3) z-curve通过将预期发现率（EDR）转换为选择权重参数，其置信区间具有名义覆盖率。

Conclusion: 元分析应转向用置信区间估计偏倚而非仅做显著性检验；z-curve在异质性高时是评估发表偏倚的可靠工具。

Abstract: Meta-analysts routinely test for publication bias, but a nonsignificant test is inconclusive because it may be a Type 2 error. In a large factorial simulation, I show that publication-bias tests have low power even with 1,000 studies once effect sizes are heterogeneous and the data do not meet the model's assumptions. I argue that the goal should shift from detecting bias to estimating it with confidence intervals. A confidence interval does not only bound the hypothesis that bias is absent; its upper bound also indicates how much bias remains compatible with the data. When the interval is wide, the upper bound does not exclude a large amount of bias, even if the formal test is nonsignificant. I then show that confidence intervals from step-function selection models are too narrow, covering the true amount of bias only about half the time. In contrast, z-curve's confidence interval obtained by transforming the expected discovery rate into a selection weight parameter has nominal coverage. Z-curve is therefore a valuable tool for examining publication bias in meta-analyses, especially when heterogeneity is high. I illustrate the implications of bias estimation with a meta-analysis of social priming and a set of applied-psychology meta-analyses.

</details>

Abstract: The wind energy industry relies on accurate power curve models to make power forecast, evaluate turbine performance, quantify upgrade, or support site-planning decisions. In this paper, we focus on site-planning power curves, i.e., we investigate how power curve models trained using turbine data on an operating wind farm can be transferred to a new, undeveloped farm. The traditional wisdom in the wind energy literature relies on distance, layout, or terrain characteristics for making cross-farm power curve transfer. Through the lens of domain adaptation, we propose a more reliable transfer learning approach for cross-farm power curve modeling. In the cross-farm applications, a domain is specified by the temporal environmental variates and spatial terrain variables. Domain adaptation is to find a capable similarity metric to adapt the domain on the new farm to that on the existing farm. Empirical results show that our domain adapted power curve consistently outperforms competing approaches by an appreciable margin for site-planning power predictions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用场内病例交叉设计，以同一比赛的非暂停分钟作为自身对照，通过差分消除所有比赛层面特征，仅调整比赛时间、比分和暂停前 momentum 状态。目标定义为处理组上的病例交叉效应（CACE），局部于观察到的暂停分钟。由于暂停分钟内无未处理分钟，平均效应仅通过时间趋势外推弱识别，而暂停-协变量交互项更稳定。

Result: 优势方 momentum 在暂停后的衰减速率与无暂停时基本相同，暂停并未加速 momentum 衰落。平均效应接近零且较小，但存在状态异质性：对于平均实力且舒适领先的球队，暂停导致约4个百分点的 momentum 损失（基线+20）。由于仅99场比赛，设计无法排除小效应，结果按效应量和相容区间解释而非统计显著性。

Conclusion: 补水暂停并未消除优势方的 momentum，而是移除了暂停期间本应持续的压力延续，这一移除在净期望进球上未留下痕迹。状态依赖性的发现仅为探索性结果，需进一步复制验证。

Abstract: The 2026 FIFA World Cup was the first to mandate hydration breaks, brief stoppages near the twenty-second minute of each half, in every match regardless of weather. A widespread claim holds that these breaks blunt the momentum of whichever side is dominant. We test the claim with a within-match case-crossover design, using non-break minutes of the same match as self-matched controls. This differences out every match-level characteristic and adjusts only for game time, scoreline, and pre-break momentum state. The apparent post-break fade is not caused by the break. The dominant side's momentum decays at essentially the same rate whether or not a break occurs, so momentum reverts through the stoppage as if play had continued. What the break removes instead is the continuation of that side's pressure during the stopped minutes, and this removal leaves no trace in net expected goals. We define the target as a case-crossover effect on the treated, local to the observed break minutes. The average effect is only weakly identified, resting on a time-trend extrapolation into a window with no untreated minutes, whereas break-by-covariate interactions are stable. The average effect itself is small and near zero. With only 99 matches, however, the design lacks the power to reject a small effect, so we read the results by effect size and compatibility interval rather than by statistical significance. The estimates are not uniform across match states: for a comfortably-leading side of average strength the implied effect is a momentum loss (near (-4) points on a pre-break level of about +20). We report this state-dependent signal as exploratory and in need of replication.

</details>

Conclusion: 本研究首次提供了Mott半金属基态的无偏证据，并表明该一维模型尽管缺乏Wannier阻挫，仍能捕捉TBG物理的若干核心特征。

Abstract: Mott physics in topological bands has recently attracted considerable attention, particularly in the context of twisted bilayer graphene (TBG). However, the essential ingredients for stabilizing this physics remain unclear. Here, we demonstrate a quantum Mott semimetal phase as the ground state within a one-dimensional spinful Hubbard model featuring only one orbital per unit cell, protected by inversion and particle-hole symmetries. We start from a two-orbital model where a localized $f$ orbital on the A sublattice hybridizes with a delocalized $c$ orbital on the B sublattice. Projecting the $f$-orbital Hubbard $U$ onto the active flat band yields a lattice model with Wannier orbitals centered on the B sublattice. Similar to TBG, a momentum-space scale $k_*$ emerges, setting the interaction range in the projected model to $1/k_*$. While the ground state is ferromagnetic with only the Hubbard $U$, introducing an inter-site antiferromagnetic spin coupling $J$ stabilizes a Mott semimetal$^*$ phase with a central charge $c=3$. Using exact diagonalization (ED) and density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) methods, we show that this phase hosts a spinful Dirac fermion coexisting with a neutral spin mode -- analogous to the fractionalized Fermi liquid (FL$^*$) phase in higher dimensions. Furthermore, breaking particle-hole (PH) symmetry via dispersion transforms the Mott semimetal into a Mott insulator, which is separated from a distinct Mott insulating phase by a continuous transition with a polarization jump of $1/2$. Our work provides the first unbiased evidence of a Mott semimetal ground state and demonstrates that this 1D model captures some essential aspects of TBG physics, despite lacking a Wannier obstruction.

</details>

Abstract: We obtain integer and fractional quantum Hall crystals as ground states of a two-dimensional electron system subject to a strong perpendicular magnetic field and a periodic potential. For certain fractional states, we show that the Hall crystal can constitute an anyon crystal, with a periodic ordering of well-defined anyons. We find that the latter states can be stabilized at odd denominator Landau level filling fractions when Landau level mixing is sufficiently weak, and near half-filling of the underlying lattice. These phases are obtained from a mean-field analysis of an effective lattice model of bosons attached to an odd number of flux quanta, which transmutes their statistics to that of electrons. In boson coordinates, the Hall crystal is a supersolid: a superfluid with charge order. Under strong interactions, vortex-anti-vortex pairs spontaneously nucleate in the supersolid, realizing a crystalline state of anyons.

Abstract: The recently observed signatures of superconductivity proximate to a fractional quantum anomalous Hall (FQAH) state in a twisted MoTe$_2$ bilayer has revitalized interest in quantum phases of matter induced by anyon dynamics. Here we show how a panoply of anyon-driven phases associated with doping the lattice ${ν=2/3}$ FQAH state can be realized as competing instabilities of a Fermi surface of charge-$e/3$ "quarks" coupled to a $\mathrm{SU}(3)_{-1}$ Chern-Simons gauge field, which is dual to the more conventional $\mathrm{U}(1)_3$ Chern-Simons-Ginzburg-Landau theory of quasiholes. For example, a range of electronic superconductors emerge from color superconductivity, under which the Fermi surface experiences a pairing instability mediated by gauge fluctuations. These include SC$\star$ phases -- where superconductivity coexists with topological order -- as well as topological superconductors displaying half-integer chiral central charges when the quarks are weakly paired. One example is a topological superconductor arising from a $p+ip$ "color-valley-locked" pairing mechanism with chiral central charge $c_-=-1/2$. On the other hand, both superconducting and non-Fermi liquid phases can emerge when the quarks form an itinerant ferromagnet, polarizing the Fermi surface to a particular combination of colors. Finally, our framework naturally accommodates the possibility of anyonic bound state formation, allowing access to phases induced by doping anyons of charge $2e/3$ as opposed to $e/3$ within the same model. Our work unifies many earlier proposed anyonic phases as instabilities of a single parent quark metal phase, distilling their emergence into a competition between superconductivity and itinerant color ferromagnetism.

Abstract: $p$-wave magnets exhibit odd-parity spin polarization in momentum space, with spin splitting that reverses under $\vec{k}\rightarrow -\vec{k}$, while preserving zero net magnetization. Abstract: We propose a general necessary condition for a spinful fermion chain with SU(2) spin-rotation symmetry to be gapped. Specifically, we prove that the expectation value of a properly defined fermionic twisting operator asymptotically approaches unity in any gapped phase with finite ground-state degeneracy, with finite-size corrections bounded by $\mathscr{O}(1/L)$. Consequently, a non-unity value in the thermodynamic limit provides a sufficient criterion for identifying gapless fermion chains. We confirm this criterion using the $s$-wave Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) Hamiltonian and determinant quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) simulations of interacting Hubbard models. We further extend the twisting-operator approach to SU($N$)-symmetric fermionic systems, where the gapped ground-state sector must be SU($N$)-singlet and $\langle \hat{\mathcal{U}}^M\rangle=1+\mathscr{O}(1/L)$ by a fermionic twisting operator $\hat{\mathcal{U}}$ with a suitably chosen integer $M$.

Abstract: In this paper, we examine the dynamical charge-Kondo-breakdown scenario proposed for cuprate superconductors within the perspective of renormalization group (RG) topology. By analyzing the coupled RG flow equations governing the effective low-energy theory, we determine the fixed-point structure, stability properties, and global organization of the flow. We find that the putative finite-coupling interacting fixed point is unstable against perturbations transverse to an invariant critical manifold. As a result, generic RG trajectories exhibit runaway behavior. To elucidate the global structure of the theory, we combine analytical solutions of the flow equations with numerical phase portraits and Poincaré compactification. This analysis reveals that the interacting fixed point exhibits a marginally relevant instability, generating an exponentially large but finite crossover scale. The resulting flow topology closely resembles anisotropy-driven runaway flows encountered in fluctuation-induced weakly first-order transitions. Within this framework, the apparent quantum-critical regime can be understood as an extended crossover controlled by a near-critical fixed point, rather than by asymptotic scale invariance. The results obtained indicate that the existence of extended scaling behavior does not, by itself, imply the presence of a stable interacting quantum critical state. More generally, our analysis demonstrates how RG topology can provide a powerful diagnostic for distinguishing genuine criticality from pseudo-critical behavior in theories of strongly correlated quantum matter.

</details>

The same is true of a related, long-standing puzzle: an anomalous maximum in the hard-axis susceptibility versus temperature. Here we develop an analytic $c$--$f$ theory of magnetic anisotropy in heavy-fermion metamagnets, identifying the mixed susceptibility $χ_{\rm cf}(T,h,p)$ as a single thermodynamic observable that unifies the anisotropic response across temperature, field, and pressure. We test this theory against primary and literature data for the heavy-fermion superconductor UTe$_2$, finding excellent quantitative agreement in the temperature, field, and pressure evolution of its magnetic anisotropy -- including a Kondo-coherence origin for the anomalous hard-axis susceptibility maximum, which we show is directly connected to the metamagnetic transition itself. Abstract: Stacking van der Waals materials provides a powerful route to engineer emergent electronic and magnetic behaviors through proximity-driven interactions. The graphene/CrSBr heterostructure has emerged as a compelling platform in this frontier, exhibiting exotic macroscopic responses-- including uniaxial surface-plasmon-polariton propagation and an unconventional quantum Hall effect-- indicative of strong interfacial electronic and magnetic coupling. However, a microscopic electronic landscape governing these phenomena has remained elusive. Here, we provide a comprehensive spectroscopic characterization of the graphene/CrSBr interface using a combination of angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), low-energy electron microscopy (LEEM), and density functional theory. We resolve a massive redistribution of interfacial charge that concurrently hole dopes graphene and populates the quasi-one-dimensional spin-polarized conduction band of CrSBr, resulting insulator-to-metal transition in the interfacial CrSBr layer. Furthermore, electronic structure of CrSBr exhibits strong momentum-dependent renormalization distinct from conventional charge doping, and theoretical modeling supported by Raman spectroscopy points to additional interfacial compressive strain. In addition, ARPES reveals a splitting-like feature in the graphene Dirac cone consistent with spin degeneracy lifting, providing possible evidence of magnetic proximity coupling. These findings provide crucial microscopic insight into the system's optical and transport responses and establish graphene/CrSBr as a versatile platform for charge-transfer control, strain-driven band engineering, magnetic-proximity coupling, and directionally confined excitations for next-generation spintronic and nanophotonic devices.

Abstract: Quantum Fisher Information (QFI) is a powerful spectroscopic tool to witness many-body quantum entanglement in solid state materials, but it is not always obvious how to relate it to other characteristics of condensed matter systems. In this study we elaborate on the meaning and interpretation of QFI in condensed matter by examining simple theoretical spin systems. We use finite sized spin systems to illustrate that QFI quantifies the momentum- dependent degree of quantum entanglement (that is the entanglement depth at a given wave vector) within a wave function. We subsequently use semiclassical frustrated spin models to show that, in the context of linear spin wave theory (LSWT), QFI quantifies the momentum-dependent degree of magnon squeezing in the ground state. In antiferromagnets with a zero-energy Goldstone mode, LSWT breaks down and QFI (and entanglement depth) diverges at the magnetic ordering wave vector. We also show examples of emergent quantum phases in frustrated spin systems that do not appear in classical phase diagrams. When these emergent phases are approached, QFI diverges across multiple wave vectors in momentum space. Taken together, QFI is not only helpful as a lower bound for entanglement depth, but serves as a momentum-resolved probe of entanglement that offers a novel perspective on quantum critical phenomena.

</details>

Abstract: We compute the relative smooth surgery structure sets of the thickenings $\mathbb{OP}^{2}\times\mathbb{D}^{k}$ of the Cayley projective plane $\mathbb{OP}^{2}$ for every $k\geq 1$ with $k\equiv 0\pmod 4$, by determining the corresponding normal invariants and surgery obstruction map. We show that the latter is not surjective and determine the $2$-adic valuation of the generator of its image. As applications, we construct infinitely many pairwise non-homeomorphic closed smooth manifolds of dimension $16+k$, homotopy equivalent to $\mathbb{OP}^{2}\times\mathbb{S}^{k}$ and distinguished by their total Pontryagin numbers; we compute the rational homotopy groups of the block diffeomorphism group $\widetilde{\operatorname{Diff}}(\mathbb{OP}^{2})$ in every degree congruent to $3$ modulo $4$; and we construct smooth $\mathbb{OP}^{2}$-bundles over $\mathbb{S}^{4}$, $\mathbb{S}^{8}$, and $\mathbb{S}^{12}$ whose total spaces have non-vanishing $\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}$-genus. These bundles yield elements of infinite order in the homotopy groups of the spaces of metrics of positive sectional, Ricci, and scalar curvature on $\mathbb{OP}^{2}$ in degrees $3$, $7$, and $11$.

</details>

Abstract: We study higher cluster tilting objects through covering functors from derived categories of hereditary algebras. The covering formalism reduces the existence problem to the equivariant problem of finding \(G\)-stable \(d\)-cluster tilting objects in \(d\)-cluster categories. For triangulated categories with finitely many indecomposable objects this gives a complete ADE existence criterion and explicit counting formulas. We also prove that, in finite Frobenius models, the endomorphism algebras of all \(d\)-cluster tilting objects are derived equivalent. Applications are given to Cohen--Macaulay finite categories, finite noncommutative crepant resolutions, and rigidity dimensions of representation-finite self-injective algebras.

</details>

Result: 1. 铺砌的忠实剖分与倾斜模之间存在一一对应关系；2. 定义的辅助代数与对应倾斜模的自同态代数同构；3. 构造了实现该自同态代数的新铺砌；4. 引入了保持倾斜模的翻转操作。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将温和代数上的倾斜模理论嵌入到带标记曲面和铺砌的几何框架中，通过忠实剖分、辅助代数和翻转操作等工具，建立了倾斜模及其自同态代数的几何刻画，为理解温和代数的表示论提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: This paper investigates tilting modules over gentle algebras and their endomorphism algebras within the framework of marked surfaces and tilings introduced by Baur and Simões. Faithful dissections of a tiling are shown to correspond to tilting modules. For a faithful dissection, we define an auxiliary algebra and prove that it is isomorphic to the endomorphism algebra of the corresponding tilting module. We also construct a new tiling realizing this endomorphism algebra and introduce a flip preserving tilting modules.

### [87] [Special unipotent representations and the coadjoint orbit method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20144)
*Shilin Yu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对线性实约化群和元典型群的弱Arthur/Adams-Barbasch-Vogan (ABV)包中的特殊酉表示给出了几何分类，通过变形量化方法构造了相应的Harish-Chandra模，并证明了这些模具有不可约的伴随环且是酉的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在弱ABV包中对特殊酉表示进行几何分类，统一构造其Harish-Chandra模，并验证Kirillov-Kostant-Vogan的轨道伴随方法哲学。

Method: 利用拟区分幂零轨道的万有覆盖的仿射化上的可缩向量丛的变形量化，结合Lusztig关于特殊片的几何猜想（已由Kraft-Procesi和Juteau-Levy-Sommers及作者证明）进行统一构造。

Result: 所有构造的Harish-Chandra模具有不可约的伴随环，并且由混合Hodge模理论的结果可证明其酉性。

Conclusion: 猜想所有ABV包的酉性可约化到对偶轨道为区分的情形；本文的方法为这一约化提供了基础。

Abstract: For any linear real reductive group or metapletic group, this article gives a geometric classification of special unipotent representations in the weak Arthur/Adams-Barbasch-Vogan (ABV) packets attached to quasi-distinguished nilpotent orbits in the Langlands or metaplectic dual Lie algebra in terms of their associated cycles. We provide a uniform construction of the Harish-Chandra modules of these representations via deformation quantization of admissible vector bundles over certain Lagrangian subvarieties of the affinizations of the universal covers of special nilpotent orbits in question, which aligns with the coadjoint orbit method philosophy of Kirillov, Kostant, and Vogan. As consequences, all such Harish-Chandra modules have irreducible associated cycles, and are unitarizable by results from the theory of mixed Hodge modules. Conjecturally, the unitarity of all ABV packets can be reduced to the case when the dual orbits are distinguished. Our approach highlights the application of Lusztig's conjecture on the geometry of special pieces, which has been proven for classical Lie algebras by Kraft and Procesi, and for all cases by Juteau, Levy, Sommers and the author.

</details>


### [88] [Extremality of principal quiver Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20336)
*Giovanni Cerulli Irelli,Evgeny Feigin,Markus Reineke*

<details>

Motivation: 研究箭图Grassmannian簇的不可约性与维数性质，并刻画其平坦不可约分支的结构。

Conclusion: 给出了箭图Grassmannian簇中平坦不可约分支的一个显式刻画。

Abstract: We study families of quiver Grassmannians, parametrizing subrepresentations of representations of Dyn\-kin quivers, which are naturally associated to a projective and an injective representation. Our main result is an explicit description of the flat irreducible locus of such a family. More precisely, we prove that a member of this family is irreducible and of the expected dimension if and only if the ambient quiver representation degenerates to the direct sum of the defining projective and injective representations.

</details>

### [89] [Equilibrium Causal Games: Separation, Identification, and the Identifiability of Cyclic Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19531)
*Faraz Dadgostari,Neda Nazemi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出均衡因果博弈（ECG）框架，将博弈论与循环因果模型结合，系统分析了在未知传感器观测下，从均衡数据中识别因果结构的可识别性边界。结果表明：在被动线性稳定模型中，未知连接和满秩未知传感导致B完全不可识别（d≥2）；非高斯性可消除源旋转模糊性；已知支持、不变传感、对齐响应和单目标干预可在特定等价类下唯一识别(H,B)；非线性传感下各向同性高斯源块在标记环境中存在隐藏扭曲，但满足正性、信息性单块变化、秩和不可约条件时，最细独立源块表示可在块置换和块内坐标变换下被识别。这些结果揭示了均衡数据支持哪些因果结论，以及哪些需要目标实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从均衡数据中推断因果结构的根本性挑战：电网、市场等系统通过反馈驱动达到均衡，其观测数据来自未知传感器，传统因果发现方法难以区分交互机制与传感模糊性。

Method: 提出Equilibrium Causal Game (ECG)框架，将博弈模型、循环因果模型、隐藏输入、传感器映射和干预规则统一建模。通过理论分析线性/非线性模型、高斯/非高斯源、已知/未知支持等不同条件下的可识别性，推导数学条件。

Result: ECG分离在给定条件下是可靠的但不完备；被动线性模型中未知连接和传感导致B完全不可识别；非高斯消除源旋转；已知支持等条件下d-1或d个目标可识别(H,B)；非线性传感下各向同性高斯块存在隐藏扭曲；在特定条件下最细独立源块表示可识别。

Conclusion: 均衡数据只能支持部分因果结论，许多结构需目标实验才能确定识别。这为实际系统（电网、市场）中的因果发现提供了理论指导，明确了何时纯观察数据足够、何时必须干预。

Abstract: Power grids, markets, and interacting populations, settle into feedback driven equilibria observed through unknown sensors. Our Equilibrium Causal Game (ECG) joins a game to its cyclic causal model, hidden inputs, sensor map, and rules for interventions and equilibrium selection; interventions edit declared objects and recompute equilibrium. Under stated conditions, ECG-separation is sound but incomplete in our examples. Back-door/half-trek routes identify observed queries. Yet for an untouched rotationally symmetric Gaussian block, second moments determine only a source-frame rotation, across which distinct-variable effects generically change. Unknown sensing creates a separate ambiguity. In passive stable linear models without self-effects, unknown wiring and full-rank unknown sensing leave $B$ completely unidentified for $d\ge2$. Under LiNG, non-Gaussianity removes the source rotation; mechanism interventions separate sensing from interactions. With unknown support, invariant sensing, aligned responses, and well-posed single-target interventions identify $(H,B)$ up to declared equivalence. Of $d$ targets, $d-1$ suffice exactly when the sole untargeted node directly parents all others; otherwise $d$ are needed. Acquisition probes are excluded; known wiring gives no universal count. With nonlinear sensing, isotropic Gaussian source blocks admit hidden twists within and across blocks in labelled environments preserving required radial laws. Conversely, under stated positivity, informative one-block changes, rank, and irreducibility conditions, the finest independent source-block representation is identified within the stated alternative class up to block permutation and blockwise coordinate changes, but not downstream mechanisms or the sensor/interaction split. Together, these results show which causal conclusions equilibrium data support and which require targeted experiments.

</details>


### [90] [Online Optimization of Difference-of-Convex Compositions with Smooth Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19553)
*Jingwei Ji,Jong-Shi Pang,Renyuan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类结构化的非凸非光滑在线优化问题（损失函数为DC函数与光滑映射的复合，可行域由同类DC约束定义），提出了一种时间平滑的近端线性算法，并基于近端残差映射定义了局部遗憾度量。证明了该残差是问题的合理平稳性度量，其不动点条件等价于一阶平稳性。分析依赖于对复合DC约束可行域的切锥刻画，该刻画具有独立意义，并允许在非凸问题中通过凸优化oracle计算每次更新。建立了局部遗憾界和内层凸子问题总数的界，并推导了连接近端残差与到平稳点距离的误差界，提供了近似平稳性的定量证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线优化方法多针对凸或光滑问题，而实际应用中广泛存在结构化的非凸非光滑问题（特别是DC复合结构），缺乏有效的在线算法和理论保证。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出时间平滑的近端线性算法（time-smoothed proximal linear algorithm），利用近端残差映射（proximal residual mapping）定义局部遗憾度量，并通过切锥刻画实现对复合DC约束可行域的处理，使每次更新可转化为凸优化子问题。

Result: 证明了近端残差作为平稳性度量的合理性（不动点条件等价于一阶平稳性），建立了局部遗憾界、内层凸子问题总数界，以及连接近端残差与距离到平稳点距离的误差界。

Conclusion: 为含复合DC约束的结构化非凸非光滑在线优化问题提供了首个具有理论保证的在线算法和分析框架，其中对DC约束可行域的切锥刻画具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: We study online optimization for a broad class of structured non-convex non-smooth problems where each loss is a composition of a difference-of-convex function with a smooth mapping, and the feasible region is defined by constraint functions of the same kind.
  We propose a time-smoothed proximal linear algorithm and a local-regret measure based on a proximal residual mapping. We show that this residual is a proper stationarity measure for the original problem: its fixed-point condition implies first-order stationarity.
  Our analysis relies on a tangent-cone characterization for a feasible region described by composite difference-of-convex constraints, which is of independent interest and allows each update to be computed via a convex optimization oracle, despite the non-convexity of the problem.
  We establish a local-regret bound and a bound on the total number of inner convex subproblems.
  We also derive an error bound connecting the proximal residual to the distance to stationarity, providing a quantitative certificate of approximate stationarity.

</details>


### [91] [Optimization models and algorithms for the Unit Commitment problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19570)
*Javal Vyas,Carl Laird,Ignacio E. Grossmann,Ricardo M. Lima,Iiro Harjunkoski,Jan Poland*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合EGRET库中替代模型与分解方法的机组组合问题求解方案，在四个基准系统上的测试表明，该方法能够显著提升计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机组组合问题是一个具有组合复杂性和潜在长求解时间需求的挑战性优化问题，现有方法在计算效率上仍有提升空间，因此需要开发更高效的求解策略。

Method: 采用分解方法，并与EGRET库中的替代模型相结合来求解机组组合问题。

Result: 在四个基准系统上的测试结果表明，该方法实现了显著的计算加速。

Conclusion: 所提出的分解方法与EGRET替代模型相结合的策略，能够有效提升机组组合问题的求解效率。

Abstract: The unit commitment problem determines the optimal strategy to meet the electricity demand at minimum cost by committing power generation units at each point of time. Solving the unit commitment problem gives rise to a challenging optimization problem due to its combinatorial complexity and potentially long solution time requirements. Our proposed solution approach utilizes a decomposition method in conjunction with alternative models from the EGRET library. Results of this decomposition approach tested against four benchmarking systems show that significant computational speed ups are achieved.

</details>


### [92] [Data-driven feedback rectification of switched linear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19578)
*Philipp Schmitz,Hannes Gernandt,Maria C. Honecker,Karl Worthmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动方法，通过矫正切换线性系统的特征结构（使所有切换子系统具有相同的特征向量集），计算稳定化状态反馈，并利用公共二次Lyapunov函数保证闭环系统的渐近稳定性，通过两个例子（包括一个航空探测器模型）验证了该方法。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决切换线性系统中不同子系统具有不同特征结构导致难以设计统一稳定化反馈的问题，旨在通过数据驱动方式实现无需精确模型即可保证系统渐近稳定的控制方法。

Method: 提出数据驱动方法计算稳定化状态反馈，该方法矫正切换线性系统的特征结构，使所有切换子系统具有相同的特征向量集，并基于此计算公共二次Lyapunov函数以保证闭环渐近稳定性。

Result: 通过两个示例（包括一个航空探测器模型）验证了所提方法的有效性，表明矫正后的特征结构能够成功计算公共Lyapunov函数并实现稳定化控制。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动特征结构矫正方法能够有效计算切换线性系统的稳定化状态反馈，在不依赖精确模型的情况下保证系统渐近稳定性。

Abstract: In this paper, a data-driven method for the computation of stabilizing state-feedbacks is proposed that leads to a rectified eigenstructure of switched linear systems. This means that all switching subsystems have the same sets of eigenvectors and the rectification allows to compute a common quadratic Lyapunov function ensuring asymptotic stability of the closed-loop system. The method is illustrated for two examples including a model of an aerosonde.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Ellipsoids: Semi-Algebraic Tightening for Chance Constraints Under Actuator Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19639)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机模型预测控制中，机会约束系统的约束收紧问题面临无界加性扰动和输入饱和的挑战，现有方法在处理这类问题时存在保守性过高的问题。

Method: 1. 利用精确的分段仿射结构处理饱和误差动态，支持非对称饱和边界；2. 通过平方和优化设计满足漂移条件的多项式李雅普诺夫函数；3. 推导有限时间概率可达集和概率终界；4. 显式优化集合几何形状以进一步降低保守性。

Result: 数值算例验证了所提设计的有效性，表明该方法能有效降低约束收紧的保守性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种半代数约束收紧方法，通过分段仿射结构、多项式李雅普诺夫函数和几何优化，有效处理了无界扰动和输入饱和下的机会约束问题，降低了保守性。

Abstract: Motivated by stochastic model predictive control applications, we present a semi-algebraic approach to constraint tightening for chance-constrained systems with unbounded additive disturbances and saturated inputs. The saturated error dynamics are handled via their exact piecewise-affine structure, which naturally accommodates asymmetric saturation bounds. A polynomial Lyapunov function satisfying a drift condition is then designed using sum-of-squares optimization, yielding finite-time probabilistic reachable sets and a probabilistic ultimate bound. The set geometry is explicitly optimized for constraint tightening, further reducing conservatism. A numerical example demonstrates the effectiveness of the design.

</details>


### [94] [On the Universality of Simple Trust-Region Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19647)
*Clemens Sirotenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了二次信赖域方法在凸性下的通用复杂度保证，揭示了基于函数间隙-模型下降估计的通用机制。基本信赖域方法在不知晓Hessian的Hölder连续性参数ν的情况下，实现了$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1/(1+ν)})$的全局复杂度；一种改进变体同时在非凸、凸和局部区域达到最优复杂度，无需任何参数先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信赖域方法虽然在实践中表现优异，但其在不同凸性条件下的通用复杂度保证尚不明确，且多数算法需要预先知道问题的光滑性参数（如Hölder指数），限制了其自适应能力。

Method: 提出一种新的函数间隙-模型下降估计技术，分别分析两种信赖域变体：(1) 基本信赖域方法（不精确子问题求解）；(2) 精确子问题求解且修改接受比率的变体。两者均使用标准二次模型和经典半径更新机制，不依赖梯度依赖半径或立方/梯度/张量正则化。

Result: 基本方法在凸性下达到$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1/(1+ν)})$全局复杂度；在非凸下保持$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$一阶复杂度；可实现Q-超线性局部收敛（ν=0）或$1+ν$阶收敛（ν∈(0,1]）。改进变体在非凸下达到最优$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-(2+ν)/(1+ν)})$复杂度，同时保持凸通用复杂度和局部Newton速率。

Conclusion: 信赖域机制在非凸、凸和局部强凸区域具有内在的自适应性，无需知晓问题参数ν或Hölder常数，为信赖域方法的实际成功提供了进一步的理论支持。

Abstract: We establish universal complexity guarantees for quadratic trust-region methods and identify a common mechanism underlying their universal behavior under convexity, based on a function-gap model-decrease estimate that appears to be new in the trust-region literature. First, we prove that the basic trust-region method with inexact subproblem solves is universal under convexity. Under a $ν$-Hölder-continuous Hessian, it attains the global complexity bound $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1/(1+ν)})$ for computing an $\varepsilon$-approximate minimizer, without knowledge of $ν\in[0,1]$ or the corresponding Hölder constant. In the nonconvex regime, the method retains the classical $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ first-order complexity bound under the usual additional bounded-Hessian assumption. With suitably vanishing inexactness, it also recovers Q-superlinear local convergence for $ν=0$ and convergence of order $1+ν$ for $ν\in(0,1]$. Second, we show that the same convex-universal mechanism applies to a trust-region variant with exact subproblem solves and a simple modification of the acceptance ratio. This variant is universal simultaneously in the nonconvex, convex, and local regimes: it attains the optimal nonconvex first-order complexity $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-(2+ν)/(1+ν)})$, while preserving the universal convex complexity and the local Newton rates. These guarantees require no knowledge of $ν$ or its Hölder constant. Both methods use the usual quadratic trust-region model and the classical radius-update mechanism, without gradient-dependent radii or model modifications such as cubic, gradient, or tensor regularization. The results show that the trust-region mechanism is inherently adaptive across nonconvex, convex, and locally strongly convex regimes, providing further theoretical support for the practical success of trust-region methods.

</details>


### [95] [Foundations of Machine-Checked Control Theory in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19727)
*Moritz Doll,Iman Shames*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个在交互式定理证明器Lean中构建的开源库，用于对控制理论进行机器验证，为网络物理系统的验证奠定基础。代表性成果包括Lyapunov稳定性理论和小增益定理的形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为网络物理系统的验证提供经过机器验证的控制理论基础，弥合控制理论与形式化验证之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在Lean中形式化Lyapunov稳定性理论（利用邻域过滤器覆盖点/集合，适用于连续、离散和混合系统）和小增益定理（将输入输出系统建模为关系，无需良定性假设）。

Result: 成功在Lean中形式化证明了Lyapunov稳定性定理和小增益定理，并构建了包含其他系统理论结果的开源库。

Conclusion: 该库为网络物理系统的形式化验证提供了可扩展的基础架构，未来计划继续扩展更多的系统理论结果。

Abstract: We introduce an open-source library for machine-checked control theory in the interactive proof assistant Lean to lay foundations for the verification of cyber-physical systems. To this end, as representative theorems, we present formalizations of Lyapunov stability theory and the small-gain theorem. First, the machinery employed for formalizing Lyapunov stability, i.e., neighborhood filters, allows stating a Lyapunov theorem that covers both points and sets and applies to continuous, discrete, and hybrid systems. Second, the small-gain theorem is proved via stating input-output systems as relations without the usual well-posedness assumption. The Lean formalization of each of these theorems is then presented. We conclude by discussing the library architecture and mentioning some of the other system theoretic results that are formalized in the library along with future plans.

</details>


### [96] [Harnessing Heterogeneous Data for Conditional Optimization via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19761)
*Jonathan Yu-Meng Li,Qinyu Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于最优传输(OT)的分布鲁棒框架，用于在目标联合数据稀缺或不可得时，利用多源异构数据进行条件优化。该框架通过构造围绕经验源分布的OT模糊集，优化最坏情况下的条件性能，并提出了三种不同的模糊集构建方式。作者推导了可处理的重新表述形式，建立了可行性条件，讨论了参数选择，揭示了各方案在鲁棒性、信息聚合和计算复杂度之间的权衡，并通过一个条件分类问题验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件优化在实践中常因目标联合分布样本有限而难以学习相关条件分布，尤其是在目标数据稀缺或条件区域观测极少时。虽然可以从多个异构来源（如不同商店、市场、人群或运行环境）获取相关联合数据，但这些来源可能存在相对于目标分布的偏差，无法简单合并使用。因此，需要一种能够有效利用多源异构数据来进行条件优化的方法。

Method: 基于最优传输(OT)的分布鲁棒框架：1) 使用OT距离构造围绕经验源分布的模糊集；2) 优化最坏情况下的条件性能；3) 提出三种OT模糊集：强制源一致性、通过权重聚合源差异、以OT重心为中心设置模糊集；4) 推导可处理的重新表述形式并建立可行性条件。

Result: 推导了三种OT模糊集对应的可处理重新表述形式，建立了可行性条件，揭示了不同方案在鲁棒性、信息聚合程度和计算复杂度之间的权衡关系。通过一个基于多商店需求和产品特征数据的条件分类问题验证了框架的实际价值。

Conclusion: 该分布鲁棒框架能有效利用多源异构数据进行条件优化，三种模糊集设计提供了不同的权衡选择，在实际应用中具有显著价值。

Abstract: Conditional optimization tailors decisions to contextual or event information, but its practical use is often limited by the difficulty of learning the relevant conditional distribution from finite samples of a target joint distribution. This challenge is especially acute when target joint data are scarce or unavailable, or when few observations fall in the conditioning region of interest. Related joint data may be available from multiple sources, such as different stores, markets, populations, or operating environments, but these sources may be biased relative to the target distribution and cannot be pooled naively. We develop a distributionally robust framework based on optimal transport (OT) for harnessing such heterogeneous data in conditional optimization. The framework constructs ambiguity sets over joint distributions using OT distances to empirical source distributions and optimizes worst-case conditional performance over plausible target laws. We propose three OT ambiguity sets that capture different ways of using heterogeneous sources: enforcing simultaneous source consistency, aggregating source discrepancies through weights, and centering the ambiguity set at an OT barycenter. We derive tractable reformulations, establish feasibility conditions, discuss parameter choices, and characterize the relationships among the formulations, revealing trade-offs between robustness, information aggregation, and computational complexity. We demonstrate the value of the framework through a conditional assortment problem using demand and product-feature data from multiple stores.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond recency and magnitude: Learning-aware uncertainty estimation for safety stock under evolving forecast-error distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19835)
*Luis Fernández-Palacios,Manuel Ceballos,Yolanda Muñoz-Ocaña*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出LOWDII方法，通过评估历史预测误差的分布影响，在不依赖参数假设的情况下，为安全库存校准筛选代表性数据，在保持目标服务水平的同时实现库存削减5.1%-22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全库存估计依赖误差大小或时效性，但一年误差不足以刻画服务风险，多年数据又因分布漂移而不可靠。需一种非参数方法识别哪些历史误差对当前不确定性分布的贡献具有代表性。

Method: 提出LOWDII（留一法Wasserstein分布影响指数），通过计算移除单个观测前后经验不确定性分布之间的Wasserstein距离，量化每个历史预测误差对校准分布的贡献，无需参数假设。

Result: 在快消品数据的离散事件仿真中，LOWDII达到目标服务水平，同时平均库存减少5.1%至22.6%，优于基准方法。

Conclusion: LOWDII帮助企业在预测过程改进时及时将更优的预测质量转化为安全库存决策，避免过时误差持续影响库存水平，提早获得不确定性降低的经济收益。

Abstract: We develop a non-parametric approach to refine forecast-error histories for safety stock estimation without relying on error magnitude or recency alone. Using forecast-error data from a fast-moving consumer goods environment, we show that one year of errors is insufficient to characterize service-level risk, while pooling several years is problematic because error distributions change over time. Non-parametric annual comparisons indicate a progressive reduction in bias and dispersion, consistent with learning in the forecasting process. We propose LOWDII (Leave-One-Out Wasserstein Distributional Influence Index), a diagnostic that evaluates the distributional influence of each historical forecast error on the empirical uncertainty distribution used for safety stock calibration. LOWDII identifies observations whose influence is disproportionate to their representativeness, separating transient distortions from persistent tail behaviour without imposing parametric assumptions. We evaluate the method in a discrete-event simulation using subsequent-year demand realizations as validation. The results show that LOWDII achieves the target service level while reducing average stock by 5.1% to 22.6% relative to benchmark methods. From a managerial perspective, LOWDII helps firms translate improvements in the forecasting process into safety stock decisions, avoiding the projection of obsolete historical errors into the future and capturing the economic benefit of lower uncertainty requirements earlier.

</details>


### [98] [Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning for $N$-Player Stochastic Differential Games with Exploratory Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19928)
*Jisheng Liu,Jing Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了N人非合作随机微分博弈的连续时间强化学习，证明了同时哈密顿最大化与吉布斯分布的兼容性等价，给出了基于最优q函数的可计算交叉偏导数充要条件，并提出了当兼容性失败时具有显式KL散度界的近似相关均衡构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单智能体强化学习的q-学习理论拓展到N人非合作随机微分博弈，建立熵正则化探索策略下的纳什均衡存在性条件与近似均衡理论。

Method: 采用熵正则化探索策略，将每个玩家的最优响应建模为吉布斯分布；通过同时哈密顿最大化定义均衡；利用q-函数框架建立交叉偏导数条件；构建坐标路径积分近似相关均衡；扩展弱鞅刻画以支持无模型算法。

Result: 证明了同时哈密顿最大化与纳什均衡的等价性；给出了可计算的交叉偏导数充要条件；在解耦与对称博弈中无条件存在纳什均衡；当兼容性失败时，构造了具有$O(1/\gamma)$局部一致KL散度界的近似相关均衡；在遍历设定下也获得了相同的渐近收敛率。

Conclusion: 建立了N人随机微分博弈中熵正则化强化学习的完整理论框架，包括均衡存在性条件、近似均衡的显式误差界，以及无模型在线/离线算法的理论基础。

Abstract: We study continuous-time reinforcement learning for $N$-player noncooperative stochastic differential games. Each player adopts an entropy-regularized exploratory policy; given the others' actions, the optimal response is a Gibbs distribution, and a Nash equilibrium requires these $N$ conditional distributions to be jointly compatible. We prove that the natural equilibrium concept -- simultaneous Hamiltonian maximization -- is equivalent to this compatibility, and establish a necessary and sufficient condition expressed as a computable cross-partial criterion on the optimal $q$-functions. Nash equilibria exist unconditionally for decoupled and symmetric games. When compatibility fails, a coordinate path integral construction yields an approximate correlated equilibrium with explicit quadratic KL-divergence bounds that vanish locally uniformly as the exploration weight $γ\to\infty$. A $q$-function framework for the $N$-player game extends the single-agent $q$-learning theory of [21], with weak martingale characterizations motivating model-free on-policy and off-policy algorithms. The framework extends to the ergodic (infinite-horizon) setting with the same locally uniform $O_R(1/γ)$ asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [99] [Optimal Placement of Docking Stations and Resident AUVs for Subsea Pipeline Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19944)
*Gabrielė Kasparavičiūtė,Pasquale Grippa,Kjetil Skaugset,Asgeir J. Sørensen,Martin Ludvigsen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段混合整数线性规划框架，用于海底管道事故响应规划，联合优化海底对接板（SDP）布设和自主水下航行器（AUV）分配，在空间不确定性下最小化最大和平均响应时间。基于挪威Johan Sverdrup油田的案例研究表明，少量精心布置的SDP即可达到最优性能，并通过成本-时间帕累托分析揭示了部署成本与响应效率之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对海底管道泄漏事故，现有方法在响应规划中未能同时考虑SDP布设位置与AUV分配的联合优化，且缺乏对空间不确定性和响应时间双重目标（最大响应时间与平均响应时间）的系统建模。

Method: 构建两阶段混合整数线性规划框架：第一阶段最小化所有潜在泄漏位置的最大响应时间，第二阶段在第一阶段确定的最大响应时间约束下，最小化平均响应时间。通过挪威Johan Sverdrup油田案例（9个SDP候选点、33条管道）进行验证，并进行成本-时间帕累托分析。

Result: 少量精心布置的SDP即可达到最优性能；成本-时间帕累托分析揭示了部署成本与响应效率之间的明确权衡关系，为设计经济且高效的海底检测网络提供了可操作的指导。

Conclusion: 该两阶段优化框架能有效指导海底管道事故响应规划，在保证最坏情况响应能力的同时优化平均性能，为构建具有韧性和成本效益的海底检测网络提供了理论支撑与决策工具。

Abstract: A two-stage mixed-integer linear programming framework is introduced for subsea pipeline incident response planning, jointly optimizing Subsea Docking Plate (SDP) placement and resident autonomous underwater vehicle allocation to minimize both maximum and average response times under spatial uncertainty. Phase 1 minimizes the maximum response time across all potential leak locations. Phase 2 reduces the average response time subject to the maximum bound. A case study based on the Johan Sverdrup oil and gas field in Norway, with 9 SDP options and 33 pipelines, shows that just a few well-placed SDPs are enough to achieve top performance. A cost versus time Pareto analysis reveals the trade-off between deployment expenditure and response efficiency, providing actionable guidance for designing resilient and cost-effective subsea inspection networks.

</details>


### [100] [Accelerated Stochastic Zeroth-Order Quasar-Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19965)
*Eméric Gbaguidi,Julien Hermant*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对仅能访问噪声函数值的随机零阶情形，研究光滑拟凸函数的无约束最小化问题。设计了零阶连续化算法，实现了与光滑凸优化加速收敛速度相匹配的收敛保证（仅依赖于拟凸性参数），并通过镜像步改进稀疏解下的维度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非凸优化中，标准加速方法依赖子空间搜索机制（需一阶信息），而零阶场景下无法获取梯度。拟凸函数作为非凸函数的重要子类，缺乏有效的零阶加速算法。

Method: 采用连续化方法（continuized method）避免子空间搜索，设计零阶连续化算法；引入镜像步（mirror step）以改善稀疏解场景的维度依赖。

Result: 算法的收敛速度与光滑凸优化加速方法相当，仅多出一个拟凸性参数因子；镜像步提升了高维稀疏问题中的维度依赖性。

Conclusion: 零阶连续化算法可在不访问一阶信息的情况下实现拟凸函数的加速收敛，为随机零阶非凸优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We consider unconstrained minimization of smooth quasar-convex functions when only noisy function evaluations are accessible through a stochastic zeroth-order oracle. For these non-convex functions, the standard acceleration method relies on subspace-search mechanisms that require first-order information, being therefore unavailable in zeroth-order regimes. In contrast, the alternative and less conventional continuized method enable to avoid such mechanisms. In this work, we design a zeroth-order continuized algorithm, leading to accelerated convergence guarantees that parallel those of smooth convex optimization up to a quasar-convexity parameter. Our method incorporates a mirror step, improving the dimension dependence when there exists sparse solution.

</details>


### [101] [Order-2 Tightness of Block-Sparse SOS Relaxations for One-Layer ReLU Network Verification with a Matching Input-Sharing Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20013)
*Godai Azuma,Sunyoung Kim,Makoto Yamashita*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对单层ReLU网络的验证问题，提出当输入共享图是匹配图且每条边满足正则秩一条件时，阶2块稀疏 SOS 松弛是紧的。该结果将无边情况的紧稀疏松弛扩展到了首个包含非平凡双单元交互的稀疏场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Azuma等人将单层ReLU网络的验证问题形式化为QCQP，并建立了无边情况和单单元设置下的紧半定松弛。本文旨在将紧性结果扩展到首个包含双单元交互的稀疏场景（即输入共享图为匹配图），因为双单元边缘块的紧性验证是核心难点。

Method: 1) 通过输入共享图表示未决ReLU在盒输入集上的共享模式，聚焦匹配图情形；2) 全局问题分解为孤立顶点块和单边块；3) 对正则秩一边，证明每个双单元局部集的凸包可由两个约化单单元凸包通过共享标量精确描述；4) 结合Azuma等人的单单元紧性结果、Farkas引理和仿射消去，为每个边块得到局部阶2证书；5) 孤立顶点块归约为盒输入集上的单单元问题，用相同阶处理。

Result: 当输入共享图是匹配图且每条边满足正则秩一条件时，阶2块稀疏SOS松弛是紧的。这首次将无边情形的紧稀疏松弛结果扩展到了包含非平凡双单元交互的稀疏设置。

Conclusion: 本文成功将单层ReLU网络验证的紧松弛结果从无边情形推广到匹配图这一稀疏设置，验证了双单元交互下紧性的可行性，为更一般稀疏图结构的紧性分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Azuma, Kim, and Yamashita formulated the verification problem for one-layer ReLU networks as a quadratically constrained quadratic program and established tight semidefinite relaxations for the edgeless case and for one-unit settings. In this work, we represent the sharing pattern of undecided ReLUs over a box input set through an input-sharing graph and focus on the case where this graph is a matching. We then derive an explicit, checkable sufficient condition for the tightness of the order-$2$ block-sparse SOS relaxation associated with the connected-component decomposition of this graph. Under the matching assumption, the global problem decomposes into isolated-vertex blocks and single-edge blocks. The key difficulty, which is absent from the edgeless case, is establishing tightness for a two-unit edge block. For regular rank-one edges, we show that the convex hull of each two-unit local set can be described exactly by two reduced one-unit hulls coupled through a common shared scalar. Combining the one-unit tightness result of Azuma et al. with Farkas' lemma and affine elimination, we obtain a local order-$2$ certificate for each edge block. Isolated-vertex blocks reduce to one-unit problems over box input sets and are therefore handled at the same order. We prove that, when the input-sharing graph is a matching and every edge satisfies the regular rank-one condition, the order-$2$ block-sparse SOS relaxation is tight. This extends the tight sparse relaxation result for the edgeless case to the first sparse setting with a nontrivial two-unit interaction.

</details>


### [102] [Forward-Reflected-Backward algorithm with Linesearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20113)
*Fernando Muñoz García,Fernando Roldán*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对单调包含问题（涉及极大单调算子与连续算子的和），提出了一种新的FRB算法线搜索策略，该策略在算子仅为连续时依然能保证收敛，且每迭代仅需一次算子评估，数值实验表明该方法在鞍点问题和图像恢复任务中具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解单调包含问题的方法（如FBF和FRB）存在局限：FBF每迭代需两次算子评估；FRB的线搜索版本仅保证局部Lipschitz算子的收敛性，对连续算子可能不终止。本文旨在设计一种对连续算子依然可行的FRB线搜索策略，同时避免全局Lipschitz常数估计和过保守步长。

Method: 提出一种新型线搜索策略集成到FRB算法中，使其在算子仅为连续时也能良好定义并保证收敛；进一步扩展算法以处理附加的cocoercive和Lipschitz连续算子。

Result: 数值实验表明：所提FRB线搜索在算子Lipschitz连续时能加速收敛；与线搜索FBF相比具有竞争力，且在多种场景下提供显著的计算优势。

Conclusion: 新线搜索策略有效解决了现有FRB线搜索对连续算子失效的问题，兼具理论保证和实际计算效率，为单调包含问题提供了更优的求解方案。

Abstract: In this article, we aim to solve a monotone inclusion problem involving the sum of a maximally monotone operator and a continuous operator. While several algorithms exist to solve this problem when the continuous operator is cocoercive or Lipschitz continuous, they typically require the estimation of the global Lipschitz constant, which can be computationally expensive and often imposes overly restrictive step-sizes. To avoid these limitations and to handle merely continuous operators, linesearch subroutines are employed. A popular method in this context is the forward-backward-forward (FBF) algorithm (also known as Tseng's splitting), which utilizes a linesearch to guarantee convergence. However, a drawback of FBF is that the continuous operator must be evaluated twice per iteration. On the other hand, the forward-reflected-backward (FRB) algorithm proposed by Malitsky and Tam (2020) requires only a single evaluation of the operator per iteration. Although a linesearch version of FRB exists, its convergence is guaranteed only for locally Lipschitz operators; in fact, we present an example demonstrating that this existing linesearch can fail to terminate when the operator is merely continuous. In this work, we propose a novel linesearch strategy for FRB that is well defined and guarantees convergence even when the operator is merely continuous. We also extend the proposed algorithm to handle additional cocoercive and Lipschitz continuous operators. Finally, we provide numerical experiments on saddle-point problems and image restoration. The numerical results show that FRB with the proposed linesearch can accelerate the numerical convergence even when the operator is Lipschitz continuous. In addition, these results show that the proposed method is competitive with linesearch FBF, offering considerable computational advantages in various scenarios.

</details>


### [103] [A Coupled Nonsmooth Dynamical System: Global Well-Posedness, Stability and Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20133)
*Shengda Zeng,Jia Chen,Vo Thanh Phat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类耦合非光滑动力系统，该系统由半线性发展方程与由时变凸集法锥控制的隐式代数律耦合而成。通过变换变量将冻结代数律重构为凸集上的变分不等式，在强伪单调、Lipschitz连续假设下证明了代数响应的唯一性及状态-控制映射的灵敏度估计。进而将耦合系统简化为单一状态变量的半线性发展方程，利用C₀半群估计与Bielecki不动点论证证明了有限时间区间上温和解对的全局存在唯一性及Hadamard适定性，并建立了增量指数稳定性判据和参数-解映射的连续性。最后通过接触力学简化算例展示了变分不等式公式及显式投影残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理一类半线性发展方程与由时变凸集法锥控制的隐式代数律耦合的非光滑动力系统，其中代数变量无法通过显式反馈获得，需从状态相关的法锥关系中恢复，这给系统的分析求解带来本质困难。

Method: 引入变换变量将冻结代数律重构为凸集上的变分不等式；在强伪单调、Lipschitz连续假设下分析代数响应唯一性与灵敏度；利用C₀半群估计和Bielecki不动点论证证明解的存在唯一性及适定性；通过加权强单调构造和Lipschitz小条件给出可验证充分条件。

Result: 获得了代数响应的唯一性、状态-控制映射的灵敏度估计（无需投影-收缩论证）；证明了有限时间区间上温和解对的全局存在唯一性、Hadamard适定性、增量指数稳定性及参数-解映射的连续性。

Conclusion: 所提出的变分不等式框架有效解决了隐式法锥代数律的耦合系统分析问题，简化接触力学算例验证了公式表述及投影残差可用于离散后的投影类和Newton型内层求解器。

Abstract: This paper studies a coupled nonsmooth dynamical system in which a semilinear evolution equation is coupled with an implicit algebraic law governed by the normal cone to a time-dependent convex set. The main difficulty is that the algebraic variable is not given by an explicit feedback, but must be recovered from a state-dependent normal cone relation. Using a transformed variable, we recast each frozen algebraic law as a variational inequality over a convex set. Under a strongly pseudomonotone, Lipschitz continuous hypothesis, this frozen problem has a unique algebraic response and admits a sensitivity estimate for the state-to-control map without a projection-contraction argument. We also give verifiable sufficient conditions for this hypothesis, including a weighted strongly monotone construction and a standard Lipschitz-smallness condition, and point out that the framework allows strongly pseudomonotone nonmonotone frozen operators. The coupled system is then reduced to a semilinear evolution equation with a single state variable. By combining $C_0$-semigroup estimates with a Bielecki fixed-point argument, we prove global existence and uniqueness of mild solution pairs on finite time intervals and establish Hadamard well-posedness through explicit continuous-dependence estimates. We further derive an incremental exponential stability criterion in the dissipative semigroup regime and prove continuity of the parameter-to-solution map with respect to the initial datum and an external parameter. A reduced contact-mechanics example illustrates the variational inequality formulation and an explicit projection residual that can be used as a starting point for projection- and Newton-type inner solvers after discretization.

</details>


### [104] [Decentralized Online Riemannian Optimization for Strongly Geodesically Convex Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20316)
*Zhanyuan Cai,Emre Sahinoglu,Shahin Shahrampour*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次在具有有界截面曲率的黎曼流形（包括正曲率流形）上，针对强测地凸损失研究了去中心化在线优化，提出了适用于时变步长的网络误差分析方法，并证明了去中心化在线黎曼梯度下降达到 $O(\log T)$ 的静态遗憾界，匹配强凸欧几里得在线优化的极小极大最优速率；同时，通过强子凸性论证，将相同界拓展到两点对偶反馈设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集中式黎曼优化中，强测地凸性可将最优遗憾从 $O(\sqrt{T})$ 收紧到 $O(\log T)$；然而在去中心化黎曼设定下，现有方法仅处理一般测地凸损失，强测地凸区域尚未被探索。核心挑战在于集中式所需的衰减步长与现有网络误差分析中固定的步长假设不兼容。

Method: 1) 提出适用于时变调度的一般网络误差分析框架；2) 基于该分析，对去中心化在线黎曼梯度下降建立 $O(\log T)$ 静态遗憾界；3) 利用强子凸性论证，将结果扩展到两点对偶反馈设定。

Result: 首次在去中心化黎曼设定下对强测地凸损失实现了 $O(\log T)$ 遗憾界，匹配了强凸欧几里得在线优化的极小极大最优速率，并将在两点对偶反馈设定下保持相同界。

Conclusion: 去中心化在线黎曼优化中的强测地凸性同样能带来遗憾从 $O(\sqrt{T})$ 到 $O(\log T)$ 的指数级改进，且该结果在带噪声的 bandit 反馈下依然成立。

Abstract: We study decentralized online optimization for strongly geodesically convex (strongly g-convex) losses on Riemannian manifolds with bounded sectional curvature, including positively curved manifolds. In centralized Riemannian optimization, strong g-convexity tightens the optimal regret from $O(\sqrt{T})$ to $O(\log T)$, where $T$ is the time horizon; in the decentralized Riemannian setting, however, existing methods address only g-convex losses, leaving the strongly g-convex regime unexplored. One challenge is that the required decaying step size in the centralized regime is incompatible with existing network-error analyses, which typically assume a fixed step size. First, we provide a general network-error analysis for time-varying schedules. Next, we build on this analysis to establish the first $O(\log T)$ static regret bound for decentralized online Riemannian gradient descent, matching the minimax-optimal rate for strongly-convex Euclidean online optimization. Finally, we prove the same $O(\log T)$ regret bound for the two-point bandit feedback setting using novel strong subconvexity arguments for the smoothed versions of the loss functions.

</details>


### [105] [Shape optimization for thermoelasticity with temperature-dependent material parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20332)
*Marc Dambrine,Helmut Harbrecht,Viacheslav Karnaev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了考虑温度依赖性材料参数的热弹性形状优化问题，推导了形状导数表达式，并通过有限元法和水平集方法分别求解了两种优化问题：在体积约束下最小化柔度，以及在von Mises应力L²范数约束下最小化体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热弹性问题中材料参数随温度变化，这类形状优化问题比常规弹性问题更为复杂，需要系统的理论分析和数值方法支持。

Method: 证明了形状导数的存在性并推导了域积分型泛函的形状导数表达式；采用有限元法求解边界值问题，使用水平集方法表示实际域。

Result: 针对两个设定给出了数值结果：（1）体积约束下最小化柔度；（2）von Mises应力L²范数约束下最小化体积。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论分析和数值方法有效解决了温度依赖性材料参数的热弹性形状优化问题。

Abstract: We consider the numerical solution of shape optimization problems for thermoelasticity with temperature-dependent material parameters. We show the existence of the shape derivative and derive an expression for generic functionals of domain integral type. Numerical results are presented for two settings: minimization of the compliance under a volume constraint, and minimization of the volume under a constraint on the $L^2$-norm of the von Mises stress. We use the finite element method for solving the underlying boundary value problems and the level set method for the representation of the actual domain.

</details>


### [106] [Lipschitzian SLLNs for random functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20411)
*Lai Tian,Johannes O. Royset*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Lipschitz伪度量下证明了局部Lipschitz函数的强大数定律，条件涵盖拓扑或模型论框架（包括o-极小结构可定义函数及其扩展），并应用于极限/Clarke次微分的均匀收敛与解的有限样本识别，从而刻画了不出现先前消极结果中失败现象的函数类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Tian & Royset, 2025）揭示了某些函数类在Lipschitz伪度量下强大数定律失效的消极现象，本文旨在刻画这些失败现象不出现的函数类别，即建立更广泛的强大数定律成立的条件。

Method: 在Lipschitz伪度量下，分别利用拓扑条件和模型论条件（涵盖o-极小结构可定义函数并显著扩展）证明局部Lipschitz函数的强大数定律。

Result: 在拓扑或模型论条件下，局部Lipschitz函数在Lipschitz伪度量下满足强大数定律；该结果进一步导出极限次微分与Clarke次微分的均匀收敛性以及解的有限样本识别性质。

Conclusion: 本文识别出广泛的函数类别，在这些类别中先前揭示的强大数定律失败现象不会发生。

Abstract: We prove strong laws of large numbers for locally Lipschitz functions in the Lipschitz pseudometric. Our results hold under either a topological or a model-theoretic condition, with the latter encompassing functions jointly definable in o-minimal structures but extending substantially beyond this class. Applications include uniform convergence of limiting and Clarke subdifferentials and finite-sample identification of solutions. Consequently, we identify broad classes of functions for which the failure phenomena revealed by our previous negative results [Tian and Royset, arXiv:2511.16568, 2025] do not occur.

</details>

### [107] [Machine Can Automatically Discover Parametric Functions to Model HEP Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19750)
*Ho Fung Tsoi,Dylan Rankin,Cecile Caillol,Miles Cranmer,Sridhara Dasu,Javier Duarte,Philip Harris,Elliot Lipeles*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出使用符号回归（SymbolFit包）自动化HEP数据分析中函数模型的迭代选择过程，在CMS和ATLAS双喷注谱上验证发现大量拟合良好的函数，并重新发现了已发表搜索中使用的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HEP数据分析中，拟合分箱数据的函数模型依赖于人工反复试错（猜测函数形式→拟合→检查→重复），效率低下且依赖直觉。

Method: 将符号回归与不确定性建模结合，开发SymbolFit包，实现数据驱动的函数空间搜索，无需预先指定函数形式。

Result: 在CMS和ATLAS Run 2双喷注谱上，560次独立种子运行在7种简单拟合配置中生成了超过1000个拟合良好的函数（χ²/NDF≈1），其中111次运行重新发现了已发表双喷注搜索中使用的函数。

Conclusion: 符号回归可有效自动化HEP数据分析中的函数建模迭代过程。

Abstract: In HEP data analyses, finding an adequate function to model binned data has largely relied on a manual process: guess a functional form by intuition, fit, examine, then repeat until successful. We show that this iterative process can be automated by a machine using symbolic regression, which performs a data-driven search over function space without requiring prior knowledge of what an adequate function should look like. We present the SymbolFit package, which pairs symbolic regression with uncertainty modeling to target HEP analysis use cases, and demonstrate it on the CMS and ATLAS Run 2 dijet spectra: 560 independent seeded runs across seven simple fit configurations generated over 1000 functions fitting the spectra with $χ^2/\text{NDF}\approx 1$, and 111 of the runs rediscovered the very dijet and UA2 functions used in published dijet searches.

</details>

Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在BESIII实验中使用约10亿J/ψ事件，验证了一种在通用谱仪中测量末态核子自旋极化的新技术，证明通用谱仪可作为大接受度偏振仪使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用谱仪仅能提供末态粒子的四动量信息，无法测量自旋极化，限制了粒子与核物理研究中自旋相关物理的探测能力。

Method: 利用BESIII收集的10.09×10⁹个J/ψ事例，测量极化质子与探测器支撑材料散射的不对称性，并与理论预期值进行比较验证。

Result: 测量得到的散射不对称性与预期值一致，证明了该技术的有效性。

Conclusion: 通用谱仪可被用作大接受度偏振仪，在提供末态粒子四动量的同时额外获得自旋极化信息，从而增强现有及未来粒子与核物理设施的研究能力。

Abstract: A novel technique for measuring the spin polarization of final-state nucleons in a general-purpose spectrometer is validated. Using $10.09\times10^{9}$ $J/ψ$ events at BESIII, the asymmetry of polarized proton scattering on detector support material is measured, and is consistent with the expected value. This proves that a general-purpose spectrometer can be utilized as a large-acceptance polarimeter, providing the spin polarization in addition to the conventional four-momentum information of the final-state particles. With this technique, physics capabilities are enhanced for existing and future facilities in particle and nuclear physics.

</details>


### [109] [First Measurement of the Relative Phase between Proton Psionic Form Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19948)

X. Zhao,Z. G. Zhao,A. Zhemchugov,B. Zheng,B. M. Zheng,J. P. Zheng,W. J. Zheng,W. Q. Zheng,X. R. Zheng,Y. H. Zheng,B. Zhong,C. Zhong,H. Zhou,J. Q. Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII上100.9亿J/ψ事件，通过测量次级散射极化这一新技术，首次确定了质子类ψ介子形状因子之间的相对相位正弦值sinΔΦ=-0.20±0.34_stat±0.11_syst，为理解质子内部结构的复杂动力学提供了直接实验依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子类时形状因子之间的相对相位是完整理解质子内部结构的关键可观测量，但由于确定末态极化或获得极化束流的巨大实验挑战，该量一直未被测量。

Method: 采用一种创新技术，通过测量次级散射在谱仪材料上的极化来实现；利用BESIII上收集的10.09×10⁹个J/ψ事件，分析e⁺e⁻→J/ψ→p\bar{p}反应过程。

Result: 首次确定了质子类ψ介子形状因子之间相对相位的正弦值：sinΔΦ=-0.20±0.34_stat±0.11_syst。

Conclusion: 该结果为质子形成的复杂动力学提供了首个直接实验洞察，为约束核子结构的理论模型提供了有价值的新信息。

Abstract: The relative phase between the time-like form factors of the proton is a crucial observable for a complete understanding of its internal structure, yet it has remained unmeasured due to the formidable experimental challenge of determining the final-state polarization or having available polarized beams. With a novel technique that measures polarization via secondary scattering on spectrometer material, we use $10.09\times10^{9}$ $J/ψ$ events collected at BESIII to analyze the reaction $e^+e^-\rightarrow J/ψ\rightarrow p\bar{p}$. This allows the first determination of the sine of the relative phase between the proton psionic form factors, $\sinΔΦ=-0.20\pm0.34_{\textrm{stat}}\pm0.11_{\textrm{syst}}$. This result provides the first direct insight into the complex dynamics of proton formation, and offers valuable new information to constrain theoretical models of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [110] [Evidence for longitudinally polarised $Z$ bosons in electroweak $W^{\pm}Z$ production in association with two jets from $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = $ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20246)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文测量了质子-质子碰撞中电弱W±Z与两个喷注的联合产生过程(WZjj-EW)中W和Z玻色子的极化态，基于ATLAS探测器收集的140 fb⁻¹(13 TeV)和164 fb⁻¹(13.6 TeV)数据。观测到纵向极化Z玻色子信号的显著性为4.0倍标准差，测得的纵向极化Z玻色子事件占比fX0=0.32±0.09，与标准模型预测一致。对纵向极化W玻色子事件占比及双玻色子纵向极化事件占比分别设定了95%置信水平的上限(0.19和0.12)。还测量了13.6 TeV下每轻子味道的积分截面，结果与标准模型预期相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验电弱WZjj-EW过程中W和Z玻色子的极化态，验证标准模型在电弱修正阶的预测，并寻找可能的新物理信号。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV和13.6 TeV的质子-质子碰撞数据，选取W±Z候选事件中玻色子衰变至电子和缪子的轻子道，通过运动学重建和极化变量分析，结合最大似然拟合提取极化分数和截面。

Result: 纵向极化Z玻色子信号显著性4.0σ，fX0=0.32±0.09；纵向极化W玻色子事件分数上限0.19(95% CL)，双玻色子纵向极化事件分数上限0.12(95% CL)；13.6 TeV下每轻子味道积分截面σ=0.374±0.077(stat.)±0.053(syst.)±0.009(lumi.) fb。

Conclusion: 所有测量结果均与标准模型预期一致，未发现偏离标准模型的新物理迹象。

Abstract: Measurements of polarisation states of $W$ and $Z$ gauge bosons in electroweak $W^{\pm}Z$ production in association with two jets ($WZjj\mathrm{-EW}$) in proton-proton collisions are presented. The data set used corresponds to integrated luminosities of 140 fb$^{-1}$ and 164 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collisions at centre-of-mass energies of 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV, respectively, recorded by the ATLAS detector at the CERN Large Hadron Collider. The $W^{\pm}Z$ candidate events are reconstructed using leptonic decay modes of the gauge bosons into electrons and muons. The observed significance for the presence in data of $WZjj\mathrm{-EW}$ events containing a longitudinally polarised $Z$ boson is of 4.0 standard deviations. The measured fraction of such events, integrated over the fiducial region, is $f_{\mathrm{X0}} = 0.32 \pm 0.09$, in agreement with the Standard Model prediction at next-to-leading-order in electroweak corrections. Upper limits at 95% confidence level of 0.19 and 0.12 are set on the fraction of events with a $W$ boson longitudinally polarised and on the fraction of events with the two bosons longitudinally polarised, respectively. The integrated fiducial cross-section per lepton flavour of $WZjj\mathrm{-EW}$ production at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV is also measured to be $σ_{WZjj\mathrm{-EW} \rightarrow \ell^{'} ν\ell \ell jj} = 0.374 \; \pm 0.077 (\mathrm{stat.}) \; \pm 0.053 (\mathrm{syst.}) \; \pm 0.009 (\mathrm{lumi.}) \; \mathrm{fb}$, where $\ell$ and $\ell^{'}$ are either an electron or a muon. This measurement is in agreement with the Standard Model expectation.

</details>


### [111] [Search for $C\!P$ violation in $D^+ \to φπ^+$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20276)

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb探测器在Run 2期间收集的6 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，对Cabibbo压低衰变D⁺→φπ⁺中的CP破坏进行寻找。测得直接CP不对称性为a_CP=(0.1±4.9(stat)±2.4(syst))×10⁻⁴，未观察到显著的CP破坏。首次在强子对撞机上研究了D⁺→K⁰π⁺与D⁺→K̅⁰π⁺衰变振幅的模之比和相对强相位，并给出了二维置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Cabibbo压低的D⁺→φπ⁺衰变中寻找CP破坏，检验标准模型对粲夸克衰变中CP破坏的预测，并首次在强子对撞机上研究相关衰变振幅参数。

Method: 利用D⁺→K_S⁰π⁺衰变样本校正粲介子产生不对称和伴随φ介子的带电π子的探测效应，并扣除中性K介子混合、再生以及Cabibbo允许与双Cabibbo压低衰变干涉等过程引入的不对称性，从而提取直接CP不对称性。

Result: 直接CP不对称性测量值为a_CP=(0.1±4.9(stat)±2.4(syst))×10⁻⁴，与零一致。首次给出D⁺→K⁰π⁺与D⁺→K̅⁰π⁺衰变振幅模之比和相对强相位的二维置信区间。

Conclusion: 在D⁺→φπ⁺衰变中未观察到显著的CP破坏信号，测量精度受限于统计量；首次在强子对撞机环境中获得了相关衰变振幅参数的约束。

Abstract: A search for charge-parity ($C\!P$) violation in the Cabibbo-suppressed $D^+ \to φπ^+$ decay is presented, using proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6\text{ fb}^{-1}$, collected at a centre-of-mass energy of $13\text{ TeV}$ with the LHCb detector during Run 2. An abundant sample of $D^+ \to K_{\rm S}^0 π^+$ decays is employed to correct for asymmetries arising from the production of the charmed meson and from detection effects associated with the charged pion accompanying the $φ$ meson. Asymmetries induced by interference of multiple processes, such as neutral kaon mixing, regeneration of neutral kaons, and interference between the Cabibbo-favoured $D^+ \to \overline{K}^0 π^+$ and the doubly Cabibbo-suppressed $D^+ \to {K}^0 π^+$ decay, are subtracted. The direct $C\!P$ asymmetry in the $D^+ \to φπ^+$ decay is measured to be \begin{equation*} a_{C\!P}(D^+ \to φπ^+) = \left(0.1 \pm 4.9\text{ (stat)} \pm 2.4\text{ (syst)} \right)\times 10^{-4}. \end{equation*} For the first time at a hadron collider, the modulus of the ratio and the relative strong phase of the $D^+ \to {K}^0 π^+$ to $D^+ \to \overline{ K}^0 π^+$ decay amplitudes are also investigated. Two-dimensional confidence intervals are reported for these parameters.

</details>


### [112] [Lightest $0^{-+}$ Glueball as Dominant Constituent of $X(2370)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20366)
*BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII收集的100亿J/ψ事件，通过J/ψ→γK_S^0K_S^0π^0过程寻找X(2370)→K*(892)^0\bar{K}^0+c.c.衰变，未发现显著信号，设定了上限。综合证据表明X(2370)是味单态，其性质与最轻0^{-+}胶球特征一致，支持最轻0^{-+}胶球是X(2370)的主要成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用BESIII收集的大量J/ψ数据，通过特定衰变道搜索X(2370)的衰变模式，以检验其是否为最轻0^{-+}胶球的主导成分。

Method: 基于100亿J/ψ事件，通过J/ψ→γK_S^0K_S^0π^0过程搜索X(2370)→K*(892)^0\bar{K}^0+c.c.衰变，未发现信号并设定90%置信水平的上限。

Result: 未发现该衰变模式，B[J/ψ→γX(2370)] × B[X(2370)→K*(892)^0\bar{K}^0+c.c.→K_S^0K_S^0π^0] < 2.7×10^{-6}（90%置信水平）。K*(892)\bar{K}模式的压制表明X(2370)是味单态，这是首个在1 GeV/c²以上观测到的味单态轻强子。

Conclusion: X(2370)的味单态性质、质量、自旋宇称、J/ψ辐射衰变高产率、与η_c相似的衰变模式、窄部分宽度以及ω和φ辐射衰变的压制等所有性质，均与最轻0^{-+}胶球特征一致，而其他解释目前不被支持。最轻0^{-+}胶球是X(2370)的主要成分。

Abstract: With 10 billion $J/ψ$ events collected at \mbox{BESIII}, the decay of $X(2370)\rightarrow K^{*}(892)^{0}\bar{K}^{0}+\mathrm{c.c.}$ is searched for via the $J/ψ\rightarrowγK_{S}^{0}K_{S}^{0}π^{0}$ process. No evidence of this decay mode is found, with $\mathcal{B}[J/ψ\rightarrowγX(2370)] \times \mathcal{B}[ X(2370) \rightarrow K^{*}(892)^{0}\bar{K}^{0}+\mathrm{c.c.} \rightarrow K^{0}_{S}K^{0}_{S}π^{0}] < 2.7\times 10^{-6}$ at the $90\%$ confidence level. The suppression of the $K^{*}(892)\bar{K}$ mode indicates the $X(2370)$ as a flavor-singlet state. It is the first flavor-singlet light hadron observed above $1~\textrm{GeV}/c^{2}$. All \mbox{BESIII} measurements on the $X(2370)$ are summarized. A dominant component of the lightest $0^{-+}$ glueball is essential for a natural and complete explanation of the properties of the $X(2370)$~\textemdash~the mass, spin-parity, high production rate in $J/ψ$ radiative decays, decay pattern similarities to that of $η_{c}$, flavor-singlet property, narrow partial decay width, and suppression of radiative decays to $ω$ and $φ$, since these properties are all consistent with the features of the lightest $0^{-+}$ glueball while other interpretations at present are disfavored. This supports that the lightest $0^{-+}$ glueball is the dominant constituent of the $X(2370)$.

</details>


### [113] [Interaction-model dependence in calorimetric energy reconstruction methods due to non-linear material effects in modern neutrino detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20419)
*Katharina Lachner,Steven Boyd,Stephen Dolan,Laura Munteanu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文评估了由于探测器材料效应（如Birks猝灭和复合效应）导致的能量重建偏差对不同中微子事件生成器（GENIE、NEUT、NuWro、GiBUU）的依赖程度，发现对闪烁体探测器偏差约7-9 MeV，对液氩探测器偏差约11-18 MeV，混合重建可降至约3.5 MeV，指出该偏差对中微子振荡和截面测量构成不可忽视的系统不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡实验依赖高精度能量重建，但探测器材料效应（闪烁体的Birks猝灭、TPC的复合效应）引入非线性响应，导致可见强子能量重建存在歧义，需要依赖相互作用模型假设来解决，这引入了额外的系统不确定度。

Method: 使用GENIE、NEUT、NuWro、GiBUU等多个中微子事件生成器的不同调谐版本，针对T2K (ND280)、NOνA、MINERνA、μBooNE、DUNE等实验的代表性情况，评估纯量热重建和理想化混合重建（径迹+量热法）下的能量重建偏差。

Result: 纯量热重建下，不同模型间平均中微子能量重建偏差差异显著：闪烁体探测器约7-9 MeV，液氩探测器约11-18 MeV。理想化混合重建可将液氩探测器的偏差降至约3.5 MeV。

Conclusion: 材料效应导致的中微子能量重建偏差对振荡和截面测量可能带来不可忽略的系统不确定度，需要采用替代分析策略来缓解该问题。

Abstract: Neutrino oscillation experiments rely on high precision neutrino energy reconstruction. A common reconstruction technique in LAr-TPCs and scintillators is via the calorimetric sum of visible particles created in the interaction. However, non-linearities in the detector response, such as Birks quenching for scintillators and recombination effects for TPCs, lead to ambiguities in the reconstruction of visible hadronic energies. Interaction-model-dependent assumptions are required to resolve these ambiguities, which introduces a bias in reconstruction of neutrino energy. This introduces a systematic uncertainty separate from the well-studied bias due to interaction-model dependent modelling of missing energy caused by, for example, the production of final state neutrons. In this work, we evaluate the interaction-model dependence of the bias caused by these material effects across multiple tunes of the GENIE, NEUT, NuWro, and GiBUU neutrino interaction event generators for cases representative of calorimetric energy reconstruction at the T2K (ND280), NO$ν$A, MINER$ν$A, $μ$BooNE, and DUNE experiments. Using pure calorimetric reconstruction, our results show significant differences in the mean neutrino-energy reconstruction bias between models, at the level of $\sim$7-9\,MeV for scintillator detectors and $\sim$11-18\,MeV for argon-based detectors in the relevant energy range. The latter is shown to be reduced (down to $\sim$3.5\,MeV) when using an idealised hybrid energy reconstruction based on tracking and calorimetry. Overall, we conclude that neutrino-energy reconstruction bias due to material effects may imply non-negligible systematic uncertainties for neutrino oscillation and cross-section measurements, and discuss alternative analysis strategies to mitigate the issue.

</details>
