### [114] [Circumstellar Disc and X-ray Variability in the Be/X-ray Binary SXP 5.05 During its 2024 Outburst](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19373)
*Chintan Patel,Sayantan Bhattacharya,Karan Akbari,Rajapandi Nadar,Sudip Bhattacharyya,Manojendu Choudhury*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对Be/X射线双星SXP 5.05在2024年的爆发进行了多波段分析，利用NICER的X射线观测和OGLE的光学监测数据，发现其X射线光变曲线强度低于2013年爆发，谱演化从软态过渡到硬态，5.05秒脉冲持续存在，光学变幅减小且存在稳定的相位锁定凹陷结构，表明变率由盘结构和观测几何变化驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SXP 5.05是一个罕见的食状X射线变率系统，其食变由Be星盘遮蔽而非恒星食引起，几何结构特殊。利用其2013年爆发已被充分研究的背景，对其2024年爆发进行多波段分析，以深入理解中子星与星周盘相互作用的时变特性。

Method: 采用NICER X射线观测和OGLE长期光学监测数据，对2024年爆发进行多波段分析。通过硬度比表征谱演化，利用脉冲分析检测相干脉动，并通过轨道相位折叠光学光变曲线研究非轴对称盘结构。

Result: （1）2024年X射线光变曲线峰值强度更低、持续时间更短，表明吸积事件减弱；（2）谱演化从软高态过渡到硬低态；（3）约5.05秒的相干脉动持续可测，性质与历史测量一致；（4）光学变幅小于2013年，可能因盘更小或密度更低；（5）轨道相位折叠光学轮廓显示稳定的相位锁定凹陷，表明非轴对称盘结构存在且在爆发各阶段演化。

Conclusion: SXP 5.05的观测变率由Be星星周盘的结构变化和观测几何共同驱动，该系统是研究Be星盘时变特性的关键目标，结合X射线和光学观测可探测盘动力学过程。

Abstract: Be/X-ray binaries provide a unique opportunity to study the interaction between neutron stars and circumstellar discs. SXP 5.05 is a particularly rare system, exhibiting eclipse-like X-ray variability attributed to obscuration by the Be star disc rather than a simple stellar eclipse. Motivated by its unusual geometry and the well-studied 2013 outburst, we present a multiwavelength analysis of its 2024 outburst using NICER X-ray observations and long-term optical monitoring from OGLE. The X-ray light curve shows a declining outburst with lower peak intensity and shorter duration compared to 2013, indicating a reduced accretion episode. The spectral evolution, characterized through hardness ratios, reveals a transition from a soft, high-intensity state to a harder, low-intensity state. Coherent pulsations near 5.05 s are detected throughout the observations, with properties consistent with previous measurements. The optical light curves show a reduced variability amplitude relative to 2013, possibly from a less extended or less dense circumstellar disc. Orbital-phase-folded optical profiles reveal a persistent, phase-locked dip structure, indicating a stable non-axisymmetric disc component that evolves across outburst phases. Together, these results support a picture in which the observed variability is driven by changes in disc structure and viewing geometry. SXP 5.05 thus remains a key system for probing the time-dependent properties of Be star discs through combined X-ray and optical observations.

</details>


### [115] [Four, One, and None: Quantifying the Ultra-High-Energy Neutrino Anomaly Across ANITA-IV, KM3NeT, and IceCube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19487)
*Dibya S. Chattopadhyay,Carlos A. Argüelles,Vedran Brdar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文联合分析了ANITA-IV的四个近地平中微子事件和KM3NeT的超高能径迹事件KM3-230213A，发现这些事件率与IceCube未探测到对应事件之间存在显著矛盾。在全天空弥散幂律流假设下，最佳拟合与观测存在~7.5σ的张力；即使考虑短时瞬变源，该张力仍达~5.9σ。结论认为，在标准模型框架内，仅靠方向和时间的涨落无法调和这些观测间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ANITA-IV报告的四个近地平中微子类事件和KM3NeT探测到的超高能径迹事件KM3-230213A所隐含的中微子事件率，与IceCube未探测到对应事件之间存在着显著的张力，亟需从统计和物理机制层面进行联合分析以检验其一致性。

Method: 构建了三个探测器（ANITA-IV、KM3NeT、IceCube）的半解析、能量和方向依赖的有效面积模型，考虑了ANITA-IV和KM3NeT的时间依赖性曝光。通过弥散全天空幂律流模型和罕见瞬变源模型进行拟合，并采用蒙特卡罗模拟对瞬变源场景进行统计检验。

Result: 弥散全天空幂律流假设下，最佳拟合与观测存在~7.5σ的张力；瞬变源场景中，若瞬变源恰好沿观测方向且在曝光窗口内出现，则张力可大幅缓解但高度调谐；若瞬变源随机分布，即使进行10^5次MC模拟，最佳配置仍存在5.9σ的张力。

Conclusion: 在标准模型框架下，无论采用弥散全天空流假设还是罕见瞬变源假设，仅靠方向和时间的随机涨落都无法调和ANITA-IV和KM3NeT的观测与IceCube零结果之间的矛盾。

Abstract: The four near-horizon neutrino-like events reported by ANITA-IV and the ultra-high-energy track-like event KM3-230213A observed by KM3NeT imply neutrino event rates that are in tension with the absence of corresponding events at IceCube. In this work, we perform a joint analysis of these events, taking into account the absence of any corresponding ones at IceCube. We construct semi-analytic, energy- and direction-dependent effective areas for the three detectors and account for the time-dependent ANITA-IV and KM3NeT exposures. For a diffuse all-sky power-law flux varying both the normalization and the spectral index, the measured event rates across the three detectors are not reproduced. The best-fit configuration, corresponding to a tension of $\sim7.5σ$, predicts approximately five IceCube events while strongly underpredicting the ANITA-IV and KM3NeT counts. In contrast to the diffuse scenario, the tension can be substantially alleviated if the events arise from short-duration transients that occur exactly along the observed directions during the ANITA-IV and KM3NeT detection windows. Such a realization, however, is highly fine-tuned. If rare transients are instead distributed randomly across the full sky over the $\sim15$-year IceCube observation period, additional sources inevitably contribute to the IceCube exposure. For benchmark populations with a probability of approximately $10^{-4}$ to produce four favorable transients at ANITA-IV, the best-fit configuration of sources, out of $10^5$ Monte Carlo realizations, remains in $5.9σ$ tension. We conclude that, within the Standard Model, directional and temporal variations alone can not reconcile the ANITA-IV and KM3NeT observations with the IceCube null result, under both a diffuse all-sky flux and a rare-transient source hypothesis.

</details>


### [116] [Shock cooling emission from late-time mass loss in low-mass He star binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19598)
*Samantha C. Wu,Anthony L. Piro*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了低质量剥离星在双星系统中的超新星爆发，发现激波冷却辐射主导早期光变曲线，部分模型可解释快速蓝色光学暂现源（FBOTs），并预测晚期射电辐射可用于检验该场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低质量剥离星在双星系统中作为超新星前身星的可能性，解释观测到的与邻近密周介质相互作用的贫氢超新星现象。

Method: 利用双星演化模拟中剥离星的质量损失历史构建周介质模型，然后数值爆炸前身星以计算超新星光变曲线。

Result: 激波冷却辐射主导早期光变曲线；部分模型光变曲线与Ib/n型超新星子类相符；一些模型演化迅速且温度较高，可自然解释部分FBOTs；双星前身星模型可产生扩展至约10^18 cm的密周介质，产生晚期射电辐射。

Conclusion: 双星系统中的低质量剥离星可产生与观测相符的相互作用超新星和部分FBOTs；晚期射电辐射可作为区分该场景的关键观测检验手段。

Abstract: A subset of hydrogen-poor supernovae (SNe) exhibit signatures of interaction with nearby dense circumstellar material (CSM). These SNe may originate from interacting binary systems, in which the SN progenitor experiences intense mass loss when it overflows its Roche lobe close to core collapse. In this work, we explore the appearance of SNe from low-mass stripped star progenitors in binary systems, for a range of initial orbital periods and masses. We model the CSM based on the stripped stars' mass loss history in binary stellar evolution simulations, then numerically explode the progenitors to calculate the SN light curves. Shock cooling emission (SCE) from the CSM dominates the early light curves, followed by SCE from the extended helium envelopes of the stripped stars, which form helium recombination plateaus. The appearance and properties of our model light curves are reflected in a subset of Type Ib/n SNe from the literature. Some of our models tend to evolve rapidly and are quite hot during SCE, so they may naturally explain some fraction of fast blue optical transients (FBOTs). Since the mass loss history of our binary progenitor models can produce dense CSM out to ~10^18 cm, interaction of this CSM with the SN shock could generate bright late-time radio emission in the years after the optical SN. Searching for late time rising radio emission from FBOTs could be used to test which events are explained by the scenario we explore here.

</details>


### [117] [No Surviving Companion to the Galactic SN 1181: Evidence for a Double-Degenerate Channel for Type Iax Supernovae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19705)
*Kohki Uno,Daichi Tsuna,Daichi Hiramatsu,Tomoya Kinugawa,Takatoshi Ko*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对SN 1181遗迹周围30角秒（约0.3 pc）范围内进行深度搜索，未探测到预期的幸存He星伴星，排除了单简并He供体通道，支持双简并通道（白矮星并合）作为其前身系统，表明Iax型超新星存在多种前身通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SN 1181被确认与弱爆炸的Iax型超新星一致，为检验单简并He供体通道提供了独特机会。

Method: 利用Gaia和Pan-STARRS1存档数据，在SN 1181遗迹30角秒范围内搜索幸存He星伴星；通过视差和自行排除Gaia源，用He星光谱模板和已知热亚矮星族群对Pan-STARRS1源进行光谱能量分布拟合；基于双星演化计算预测伴星的最小绝对g波段星等。

Result: 所有Gaia源被排除；Pan-STARRS1源的光谱能量分布与He星模板和热亚矮星均不一致；探测极限（Mg > 8 mag）远高于预期伴星星等（Mg ≲ 6.5 mag），未探测到任何He星或明亮富氢伴星。

Conclusion: SN 1181的前身系统排除单简并He供体通道，支持双简并通道（白矮星并合）；结合SN 2012Z中识别出的明亮He星伴星，证实Iax型超新星存在多种前身通道。

Abstract: Type Iax supernovae (SNe Iax) are the recently-established, yet peculiar subclass of thermonuclear supernovae, whose progenitor systems and explosion mechanisms remain debated. A leading scenario is a single-degenerate channel, in which a white dwarf accreting from a He-star companion undergoes a pure deflagration, leaving both a bound remnant and surviving companion. The Galactic SN 1181, whose distinctive properties are consistent with an SN Iax involving a weak explosion and bound white dwarf remnant, offers a unique opportunity to test this scenario. Here, we perform a deep search for a surviving He-star companion, a hallmark of the single-degenerate He-donor channel, within $30^{\prime\prime}$ ($\sim 0.3$ pc) of the remnant using archival Gaia and Pan-STARRS1 data. The parallaxes and proper motions exclude all Gaia sources, while the spectral energy distributions of the remaining Pan-STARRS1 sources are inconsistent with both the He-star spectral templates and known hot-subdwarf population. Binary evolution calculations predict a minimum mass of a companion with an absolute $g$-band magnitude of $M_{g} \lesssim 6.5$ mag, much brighter than the Pan-STARRS1 detection limit of $M_{g} > 8$ mag. Our non-detection rules out He-star and luminous hydrogen-rich companions for SN 1181, favoring a double-degenerate channel (i.e., a white dwarf merger). Adding to the luminous He-star companion identified in the pre-explosion imaging of SN 2012Z, our results provide direct evidence for multiple progenitor channels leading to SNe Iax.

</details>


### [118] [Peculiar Behavior of Optical Polarization in Blazar 1ES 1959+650: Role of Jet Magnetic Field and Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19869)
*A. Singh,A. Tolamatti,K. K. Singh,A. C. Gupta,K. K. Yadav*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对耀变体1ES 1959+650长达十年的光学观测，研究了其V和R波段光变曲线与线性偏振度的关联。发现强耀斑期间偏振度变化不大，但耀斑前后偏振度与流量分别呈正相关和负相关。作者利用螺旋磁场加横向激波模型（解释耀斑前后）以及湍流多区模型（解释耀斑期间）成功解释了这些观测现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解耀变体喷流中磁场拓扑结构和电子加速机制尚不清楚的问题，通过研究长时间尺度上的光学偏振行为来揭示磁场在喷流中的作用。

Method: 分析了耀变体1ES 1959+650长达十年的光学V和R波段光变曲线及500-700 nm波段的线性偏振度数据，通过流量与偏振度的相关性分析，并结合螺旋磁场+横向激波模型（耀斑前后）和湍流多区模型（耀斑期间）进行解释。

Result: 发现强耀斑期间V和R波段流量显著变化但线性偏振度基本不变；耀斑前偏振度与流量呈正相关，耀斑后转为负/反相关。

Conclusion: 耀变体喷流中的磁场结构可描述为螺旋场加横向激波（解释耀斑前后的相关行为），而耀斑期间的偏振特性则需由湍流多区模型来解释，表明不同活动阶段磁场拓扑和辐射区域结构存在差异。

Abstract: Measurements of high degree of optical polarization from blazars provide a strong evidence for the optically thin synchrotron radiation from the relativistic leptons in the jet. This in turn characterizes the presence of a partially ordered magnetic field in the emission region. However, the topology of magnetic field and acceleration of electrons to ultrarelativistic energies within the blazar jet are poorly understood. In this work, we investigate the decade long optical light curve of the well-known blazar 1ES 1959+650 to probe the role of jet magnetic field in the peculiar behavior of the highly varying degree of linear polarization in the wavelength band 500- 700 nm. The optical light curves in V and R-bands exhibit a strong flare without any significant change in the contemporaneous degree of linear polarization. However, the degree of linear polarization shows strong positive and negative/anti-correlations with the V and R-band fluxes measured during the epochs before and after the flare, respectively. This can be satisfactorily explained using the helical magnetic field and transverse shock under the framework of a relativistic jet with modestly varying orientations. During the flare, the fractional linear polarization is explained by the turbulent emission multi-zone model.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用MAGIC望远镜和CTAO LST-1对两个双黑洞并合候选体（GW240615_113620和GW241125_010116）进行的甚高能伽马射线观测。两个事件均未在GeV-TeV波段探测到信号，但由于前者具有极小的定位区域，后者与短暴相关联，为研究双黑洞并合电磁辐射提供了前所未有的机会。文章讨论了两种预测可探测伽马射线辐射的理论模型及未来应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用MAGIC+LST-1联合立体观测模式的高灵敏度，对两个特殊的双黑洞并合候选体进行甚高能伽马射线后随观测，以检验是否存在与引力波事件相关的TeV级电磁辐射，并验证相关理论模型。

Method: 采用MAGIC望远镜与CTAO LST-1联合立体观测模式，对两个引力波事件进行后随观测；由于观测条件不利，采用非标准分析流程处理数据。

Result: 在两个双黑洞并合候选体的GeV-TeV伽马射线波段均未探测到信号。

Conclusion: 尽管未探测到伽马射线信号，但这两个事件（极小定位区域的GW240615_113620和与短暴关联的GW241125_010116）为研究双黑洞并合的高能电磁辐射提供了前所未有的机会。两种预测可探测伽马射线的理论模型仍需未来进一步的观测验证。

Abstract: We present very-high-energy gamma-ray observations of two binary black hole merger candidates, GW240615_113620 and GW241125_010116, performed with the Major Atmospheric Gamma Imaging Cherenkov (MAGIC) telescopes and the first Large-Sized Telescope of the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory's (CTAO LST-1). GW240615_113620 was the best localized event of the fourth observing run of the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA gravitational waves interferometers. GW241125_010116 was temporally and spatially coincident with a sub-threshold short-duration burst detected with the Swift-Burst Alert Telescope (BAT), the Swift-X-Ray Telescope (XRT) and the Einstein Probe Follow-up X-ray Telescope (FXT). We observed the two events in stereoscopic mode, taking advantage of the improved sensitivity of joint MAGIC+LST-1 observations. No detection was achieved in the GeV-TeV gamma-ray band for any of the two sources. The unfavourable observing conditions of both events posed a challenge for a standard analysis and therefore a non standard analysis was necessary for both objects. Owing to the small localization area and the association with a GRB-like burst respectively, these events represented an unprecedented opportunity to study in details the electromagnetic emission from binary black holes merger events and, in particular, we discussed two theoretical models that predict a detectable gamma-ray emission and the possible future applications.

</details>


### [120] [Shock acceleration in vortex driven magnetic fields of black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20209)
*Zaza N. Osmanov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了超大质量黑洞磁层中激波对粒子的加速过程，重点分析了涡旋驱动磁场条件下的相对论性激波加速。研究发现，对于典型质量（10^8太阳质量）的超大质量黑洞，质子可被加速到数百PeV，而电子最高可达约120 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超大质量黑洞磁层中涡旋驱动磁场下，激波对粒子的加速机制及其所能达到的最大能量。

Method: 研究了一阶费米加速过程在超大质量黑洞磁层中相对论性激波处的应用，将同步辐射和逆康普顿散射作为限制粒子最大能量的主要能量损失机制。

Result: 对于10^8太阳质量黑洞，质子可被加速至数百PeV，电子可达约120 GeV。

Conclusion: 超大质量黑洞磁层中的涡旋驱动磁场激波能有效加速粒子，且质子和电子的 attainable 能量存在显著差异。

Abstract: The aim of this work is to investigate particle acceleration by shock waves in the magnetospheres of supermassive black holes, where the magnetic field is vortex-driven. For this purpose, we investigated the first-order Fermi acceleration process at relativistic shocks in the magnetospheres of supermassive black holes. In our analysis, we considered synchrotron radiation and inverse Compton scattering as the principal energy-loss mechanisms limiting the maximum attainable particle energy. We investigated the acceleration of both protons and electrons and demonstrated that, for a typical supermassive black hole with a mass of $10^8$ solar masses, protons can be accelerated to energies of several hundred PeV, whereas electrons can attain energies of up to approximately 120 GeV.

</details>


### [121] [Identifying Kilonovae in the Presence of Optical Afterglow for the Wide-Field Survey Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20233)
*Huan Yang,Zhengyan Liu,Liang-Duan Liu,Wen Zhao,Zigao Dai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文评估了WFST在双中子星并合后识别千新星信号的能力，发现识别效率主要受距离限制，在约600 Mpc内可达80%以上，预计年识别1-16例；并提出优先在首夜进行g/r波段高频率监测、次夜起加入z波段的分阶段观测策略，以利用千新星的红超特征提升识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千新星识别常被主导的同步辐射余辉所掩盖，现有方法难以有效区分两者，亟需系统评估WFST在此复合瞬变源中的识别性能并优化观测策略。

Method: 基于Fisher信息矩阵构建数值框架，模拟两种场景（AT2017gfo模板模型和物理采样千新星多样种群），各进行10,000次实现，分析距离、余辉微观物理参数等变量对识别效率的影响。

Result: 识别效率主要受源距离限制，对余辉微观参数变化不敏感；AT2017gfo类似事件在~600 Mpc内效率超80%；WFST年识别量约1-16例；颜色滤波器鉴别力在合并后第二夜迅速饱和进入稳定平台期。

Conclusion: 提出分阶段观测策略：首夜高频率g/r波段监测，次夜起加入z波段，利用千新星红超特征提升识别精度。该策略为WFST在多信使时代高效识别千新星提供了物理依据。

Abstract: Identifying kilonovae associated with binary neutron star mergers is often complicated by the presence of a dominant synchrotron afterglow. In this work, we evaluate the performance of the Wide-Field Survey Telescope (WFST) in identifying kilonova signals in composite afterglow-kilonova transients. Using a numerical framework based on the Fisher information matrix, we simulate $10,000$ realizations for each of two scenarios: an AT2017gfo-based template model and a physically sampled population that accounts for kilonova diversity. Our results indicate that kilonova identification is primarily limited by source distance. In both scenarios, the identification efficiency is largely insensitive to variations in afterglow microphysical parameters and exceeds $80\%$ at distances within approximately $600~\rm Mpc$ for AT2017gfo-like events. Under our adopted assumptions, we estimate that WFST could identify approximately $1$--$16$ kilonovae per year. Furthermore, we find that the discriminating power of color-based filters rapidly saturates, reaching a stable plateau by the second night after the merger. We therefore propose a staged observing strategy that prioritizes high-cadence $g$ and $r$-band monitoring during the first night and incorporates the $z$ band from the second night onward. This strategy improves the identification precision by exploiting the increasingly prominent red excess produced by the kilonova. Our results provide a physical basis for optimizing WFST observing resources to efficiently detect and characterize kilonovae in the multimessenger era.

</details>


### [122] [Long-lived intermittent accretion disks in the jittering jets explosion mechanism (JJEM) of core-collapse supernovae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20314)
*Noam Soker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究核心塌缩超新星（CCSN）中产生1-3对高能喷流的间歇性长寿命吸积盘的形成机制。在抖动喷流爆炸机制（JJEM）框架下，作者发现粘性驱动的角动量输运和喷流阻止极向吸积这两个正反馈过程可以延长吸积盘寿命，即使零角动量物质持续注入也能维持。该研究增强了JJEM作为CCSN主要爆炸机制的解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于对CCSN遗迹的观测发现，部分遗迹形态由1-3对高能喷流主导，这表明存在长寿命的间歇性吸积盘。作者试图在JJEM框架下解释这种特殊形态的物理机制。

Method: 在JJEM框架下，分析吸积盘中粘性驱动的角动量输运过程，以及喷流阻止极向物质吸积的正反馈效应，论证这些机制如何延长间歇性吸积盘的寿命，使其能够发射1-3对高能喷流。

Result: 发现了两个正反馈过程：（1）粘性驱动的角动量输运可延长吸积盘寿命，即使零角动量物质持续供给；（2）喷流阻止极向物质吸积，进一步延长盘寿命。此外，角动量波动的累积在某些情况下可产生净正角动量。

Conclusion: 该研究丰富了JJEM能够解释的CCSN遗迹形态多样性，增强了JJEM作为CCSN主要爆炸机制的地位。

Abstract: Motivated by observations of core-collapse supernova (CCSN) remnants that suggest cases where one to three energetic pairs of jets dominate the CCSN remnant morphology and, hence, the CCSN explosion energy, I examine the formation of long-lived intermittent accretion disks that launch such pairs of energetic jets in the framework of the jittering-jets explosion mechanism (JJEM). In the JJEM, pairs of jets explode all CCSNe. In most CCSNe, stochastic angular momentum fluctuations in the convective zones of the pre-collapse core seed instabilities above the newly born neutron star that lead to the formation of intermittent accretion disks. These disks launch several to about twenty pairs of jets that explode the star. CCSNRs with signatures of 1-3 very energetic pairs of jets require long-lived, intermittent accretion disks. I show that viscosity-driven angular momentum transport in the disk can prolong its lifetime even when material with zero angular momentum continues to feed the disk. In addition, the jets prevent matter from accreting from the polar direction, thereby extending the disk's lifetime. These two positive feedback processes, and the fluctuations that, in some cases, add up to a positive angular momentum, can substantially prolong the lifetime of 1-3 intermittent accretion disks (or none), which then launch the energetic pairs of jets. This study adds to the wide variety of morphologies that the JJEM can explain, hence strengthening the JJEM as the primary explosion mechanism of CCSNE.

</details>


### [123] [Probing the High-Energy Emission of the VHE-emitting Changing-Look Blazar B2 1420+32](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20375)
*Anjum Peer,Zahir Shah,Sikandar Akbar,Bari Maqbool,Ranjeev Misra*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对变脸耀变体B2 1420+32进行了多波段时域和能谱研究，发现其γ射线峰值流量约为4FGL-DR4平均值的60倍，光变行为表现出能量依赖性。通过SSC+EC模型成功描述了其宽带能谱分布，发现较亮状态需要更大的体洛伦兹因子和更高的喷流功率，而磁场变化较小，表明流量演化由多普勒增亮和喷流能量学共同驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对变脸耀变体B2 1420+32进行多波段时域和能谱研究，以理解其剧烈光变行为、辐射机制以及不同波段之间的关联性，特别是验证SSC、EC和SSC+EC等辐射模型对该源的适用性。

Method: 利用Fermi-LAT、Swift-XRT和Swift-UVOT数据（MJD 58818-60721），进行多波段时域分析（包括分数变率、通量-谱指数关系、波段间相关性），X射线能谱拟合（log-parabola模型），以及宽带SED建模（SSC、EC、SSC+EC三种场景）。

Result: 峰值γ射线流量约为4FGL-DR4平均值的60倍；分数变率呈能量依赖性（γ射线最大，光学/UV中等，X射线最低）；γ射线与光学/UV强相关，与X射线中等相关；X射线谱最佳拟合为log-parabola且负曲率表明X射线波段采样了同步辐射尾部与逆康普顿分量的过渡区；SSC+EC模型提供了最自洽的描述，种子光子温度约10^3 K指向红外尘埃环起源；较亮状态需要更大体洛伦兹因子和更高喷流功率。

Conclusion: B2 1420+32的变脸行为由多普勒增亮和喷流能量学共同驱动，SSC+EC模型是对其高能辐射最自洽的描述，X射线波段处于同步辐射与逆康普顿分量的过渡区。

Abstract: We present a multi-wavelength temporal and spectral study of the changing-look blazar B2~1420+32 using \emph{Fermi}-LAT, \emph{Swift}-XRT, and \emph{Swift}-UVOT data from MJD~58818--60721. The source reached a peak 0.1--300~GeV photon flux of $(4.62 \pm 0.29) \times 10^{-6}\,\mathrm{ph\,cm^{-2}\,s^{-1}}$ around MJD~60488, about 60 times the 4FGL-DR4 average, during which the photon index hardened to $2.19 \pm 0.14$; the flux--index evolution shows only weak evidence for global harder-when-brighter behaviour. The fractional variability is strongly energy dependent, largest in $γ$-rays, substantial in the optical/UV, and low in X-rays. Strong $γ$-ray--optical/UV correlations and a moderate $γ$-ray--X-ray correlation indicate that the X-ray emission tracks the $γ$-ray variability less closely than the optical/UV emission. The X-ray spectra are best described by a log-parabola, and the negative curvature measured in four of the five states suggests that the X-ray band samples the transition between the high-energy tail of the synchrotron component and the onset of the inverse-Compton component. We identified five activity states and modelled the high-energy (X-ray and $γ$-ray) component of their broadband spectral energy distributions (SEDs) using synchrotron self-Compton (SSC), external Compton (EC), and SSC+EC scenarios. The SSC-only and EC-only models either require physically disfavoured parameters or fail to reproduce the VHE emission, whereas SSC+EC provides the most self-consistent description, with a seed-photon temperature of $\sim 10^{3}$~K favouring an infrared torus origin. The brighter states require larger bulk Lorentz factors and higher jet powers, while the magnetic field varies only modestly, indicating that the flux evolution is governed by a combination of Doppler boosting and jet energetics.

</details>


### [124] [Pulsar glitches in the presence of vortex traps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20398)
*Anantharaman Sekharipuram Viswanathan,Dipankar Bhattacharya,M. Ali Alpar,Erbil Gügercinoğlu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Barnes-Hut近似算法，将中子星内部超流体涡旋的模拟规模从约10³提升至10⁵个，研究了非均匀钉扎条件下的脉冲星自转突变（glitch）机制。模拟发现：老年脉冲星（如Vela）的glitch上升呈现阶梯状特征，源于星壳裂纹形成的涡旋陷阱网络；年轻至中年脉冲星（如PSR J0537-6910）的星震导致新陷阱形成和旧陷阱释放，可产生glitch尺寸分布的双峰性；即使在均匀钉扎条件下，涡旋分布也会自发形成宏观不均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅模拟少量（约10³个）涡旋，且假设均匀钉扎，与真实中子星内部复杂结构差距较大。本文旨在通过提升模拟规模至10⁵个涡旋，探索非均匀钉扎（如星壳裂纹形成的陷阱网络）对脉冲星glitch行为的影响。

Method: 采用Barnes-Hut近似算法（一种用于多体引力问题的树状近似方法）加速涡旋动力学模拟，使大规模（10⁵个）超流涡旋的数值计算在合理时间内可行。模拟中引入非均匀钉扎势场，模拟星壳裂纹导致的涡旋陷阱网络，并分别针对老年脉冲星（Vela型）和年轻/中年脉冲星（PSR J0537-6910型）两种场景进行数值实验。

Result: 1. 非均匀钉扎条件下，老年脉冲星的glitch上升呈现阶梯状（staggered）特征，这是陷阱网络的明确信号；2. 星震触发的多陷阱同时释放可导致glitch尺寸分布出现双峰性，与PSR J0537-6910的观测特征一致；3. 即使在均匀钉扎条件下，涡旋分布也会自发产生宏观不均匀性。

Conclusion: 大规模涡旋模拟证实了非均匀钉扎（尤其是星壳裂纹形成的陷阱网络）对脉冲星glitch行为的关键影响，为解释Vela和PSR J0537-6910的观测特征提供了理论支持。此外，均匀钉扎下涡旋的自发不均匀性暗示了更复杂的动力学机制。

Abstract: Pulsar glitches are thought to originate when angular momentum is transferred to the crust of the neutron star from the superfluid enclosed within, mediated by vortex avalanches. This idea has been qualitatively validated in the existing literature by simulating a star with a small number ($\sim 10^{3}$) of superfluid vortices, subject to deceleration and uniform pinning. Here, we employ the Barnes-Hut approximation to simulate up to $10^{5}$ vortices in a reasonable time. Using the new setup, we probe glitches that originate in the presence of inhomogeneous pinning. The inner crust of a neutron star is expected to crack as the star spins down, relieving stresses and resulting in rearrangements within the crustal lattice. These regions become centres for pinning with large pinning energies. Many such vortex traps are expected to exist in mature pulsars like Vela. We simulate one such star and find that the rise of the glitch is now staggered, a clear signature of the trap network. In young to middle-aged stars like PSR J0537-6910, we expect quakes which result in new traps and also unpin the vortices in existing ones. We observe that such a configuration involving the simultaneous release of several traps could introduce a bimodality in the glitch-size distribution, a feature that has previously been reported for PSR J0537-6910. Our simulations also indicate that macroscopic inhomogeneities in the distribution of vortices could spontaneously develop in a star with uniform pinning sites.

</details>
